- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.13.0)): `graphs.Neo4j` (the default) and `graphs.InMemoryGraph` sinks, and `chronicle.WithDefaultGraphSinkType`.
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.13.0)): `eventsequences.ConstraintIndexUnavailable`: an append the Kernel refuses while a unique-constraint index rebuilds is reported as a rejection that stored nothing and can be retried.
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.13.0)): Key read-model dictionary entries by event content, event context or the causing user, and remove entries with `RemoveKey`.
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.13.0)): Set values only once with `MapOnce`, `ContextOnce`, `ValueOnce` and their every/all variants, or `once=true` in model-bound tags.
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.13.0)): Map the causing user into read models with `CausedBy`, `CausedByOnce` and `EveryCausedBy`.
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.13.0)): Insert children at the start with `Prepend()` or keep them sorted with `OrderBy` and `OrderByDescending`, also available as model-bound tags, completing projection dictionary, set-once and child-ordering parity with the C# client (#127)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.152.0)): `AddCratisChronicleUsingConnectionString` registers the client from a named connection string. (part of #5778)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.152.0)): The kernel reads storage connection details from a file through `ConnectionDetailsFile`, and infers the storage type from the connection string at startup. (part of #5778)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.152.0)): Clients report their startup state and connect in the background. (part of #5778)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.184.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.184.0)): `StateAwareEmissionGuard<TState>`, the emission-guard counterpart of `StateAwareAuthorizationPolicy<TState>`. It re-reads the recorded state on each guarded emission and ends the subscription when the caller is no longer entitled. It fails closed: a missing key, a missing instance, a failing read or a failing decision all end the subscription, reported through `OnDenied` with the same `StateDenialReason`. (#3766)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.183.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.183.0)): `IQueryRowNarrowing` and the `QueryRowNarrowing<TReadModel, TKey>` base. A narrowing supplies a predicate over the read model; Arc applies it before sorting, paging and counting, so a store-backed `IQueryable` filters in the store.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.183.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.183.0)): `UnsupportedQueryRowNarrowing`, thrown when a narrowed query returns a result Arc cannot narrow (such as an observable query), so rows are never returned unnarrowed. (part of #3765)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.14.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.14.0)): `narrow <Policy>` on a collection query. The policy is evaluated once per row with the row's own properties as the artifact; a denied row, or a row with no recorded state, is dropped. `authorize` still refuses the whole call and composes with `narrow`. (#1234)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.14.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.14.0)): ESM v47, selected only by a model that declares `narrow`; every earlier model keeps its bytes and revision. The reference evaluator applies the narrowing before a `then query` assertion compares rows, in both the C# and TypeScript compilers.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.13.0)): A policy declares typed inputs such as `asset AssetId`, a read takes its key with `by asset`, and `authorize PlanGrants` carries a mapping block such as `asset = vesselId`. An input that is not mapped is filled from the artifact property of the same name, so existing state policies keep working. Both compilers parse and print it, and the reference evaluator and specifications resolve the key through the mapping. (#1231)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.13.0)): ESM v46, selected only by a model that declares an input or a mapping; every earlier model keeps its bytes and revision. The canonical JSON, the published `esm-v46` schema, the golden and the documentation describe it.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.12.0)): ESM v45. `contains` over a required text collection or text property and `starts with` over a text property are admitted in policy state conditions, against a text constant. Both are ordinal and case-sensitive, an absent value never matches, and `not` composes with them. Only a model that uses one selects v45; every earlier model keeps its bytes and revision. (#1232)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.12.0)): The reference evaluator, canonical JSON, published `esm-v45` schema, golden and documentation cover the operators, with specifications for membership, prefixes, absence and negation.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.151.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.151.0)): Added an opt-in command to the Chronicle server, `--migrate-encryption-keys`, that copies unambiguous legacy Vault encryption keys to the current key layout. It supports `--dry-run`, verifies the result, is safe to repeat and leaves the legacy keys in place; entries that cannot be migrated safely are reported for operator review (#5746)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.151.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.151.0)): Added a configurable mode that refuses to start while legacy Vault encryption keys remain; the default stays warn-and-continue (#5746)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.151.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.151.0)): Added documentation for the key migration, its exit codes and required Vault permissions, and a staging procedure for verifying it before production (#5746)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.11.0)): ESM v44. A model with a policy that declares `reads` and a state condition such as `plan.dataQuality == true` selects it; every earlier model keeps its bytes and revision. The canonical JSON, the published `esm-v44` schema and the golden describe it. (part of #1233)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.11.0)): The reference evaluator and specification runner read the given read-model state through the policy. An absent instance denies, and a specification expresses the outcome with `given readmodel` and `then denied`.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.10.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.10.0)): Policies accept `reads` lines and a state condition such as `plan.dataQuality == true` or `article.author == $identity.id`. Both compilers parse and print them, and the syntax tree carries them as `StateConditionSyntax`. A read that finds nothing denies. (part of #1230)
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.0)): Added Chronicle and Arc configuration skills that cover the important settings, their defaults, and when and how to change them, plus development, production and troubleshooting references (part of #1091)
- **cli** ([Release v3.126.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.126.0)): `cratis build service install`, `uninstall` and `status` run a project's build loop as a user service that starts at sign-in and restarts 30 seconds after it stops: a launchd agent on macOS and a systemd user unit on Linux. `install` takes the options of `cratis build run`; the service gets the `PATH` of the installing shell and no credentials. See [cratis build service](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/blob/main/Documentation/reference/build.md#service). (part of Cratis/StudioIssues#1352)
- **AI** ([Release v7.149.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.149.0)): The reverse-engineering skill has a process-drift mode: each derived model records a watermark (the target commit or version it describes), and a newer target revision re-derives only the flows the diff touches, producing a model delta and a list of behavior changes. A `drift-scope.mjs` script maps a target diff onto the model's flows (part of #1056)
- **AI** ([Release v7.148.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.148.0)): A `published-contracts.md` rule: before changing behavior at a trust boundary or wire/protocol surface, find the published contract and its conformance vectors and cite them in the issue and pull request (or state that none exists); when a change conflicts with a vector the contract wins, so change the contract through its owner first; repositories that implement a contract run its pinned vectors as executable specs in CI (#1087)
- **Chronicle.TypeScript** ([Release v6.61.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.TypeScript/releases/tag/v6.61.0)): Add `.setOnce(property)` to fluent projections and the `setOnce` option to `@setFrom` and `@setFromContext`, so a property keeps the first value it receives.
- **Chronicle.TypeScript** ([Release v6.61.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.TypeScript/releases/tag/v6.61.0)): Add dictionary keys to fluent projections, so events can project into keyed dictionary entries.
- **Chronicle.TypeScript** ([Release v6.61.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.TypeScript/releases/tag/v6.61.0)): Add `.toCausedBy()` to map a property from the identity that caused the event.
- **Chronicle.TypeScript** ([Release v6.61.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.TypeScript/releases/tag/v6.61.0)): Add `.prepend()`, `.orderBy()` and `.orderByDescending()` to children in fluent projections and the matching `prepend`, `orderBy` and `orderByDescending` options to `@childrenFrom`, so new children can be placed first or sorted (#282)
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.21.0)): `set_once:` sets a property or dictionary entry only while it has no value, so the first event wins.
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.21.0)): Dictionary entries keyed by a value from the event: key sources `{:event_content, field}`, `{:event_context, field}` and `{:caused_by, :subject | :name | :user_name}`, with set, set once, add, subtract, count, increment, decrement and remove operations.
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.21.0)): `{:caused_by, :subject | :name | :user_name}` maps a property from the identity that caused the event; a bare `:caused_by` still reads the event field of that name.
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.21.0)): In-memory projection testing evaluates set once and dictionary entries the way the kernel does.
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.21.0)): `children` takes `prepend: true` to insert new children first, or `order_by: :field`, `[asc: :field]` or `[desc: :field]` to insert them sorted, matching the .NET client's `Prepend()`, `OrderBy()` and `OrderByDescending()` (#216)
- **Chronicle.Kotlin** ([Release v7.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Kotlin/releases/tag/v7.8.0)): Key dictionary entries on a read model from an event property, the event context or who caused the event, with `DictionaryKey.fromEvent`, `fromContext` and `fromCausedBy` on `set`, `count`, `increment`, `decrement` and `add`.
- **Chronicle.Kotlin** ([Release v7.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Kotlin/releases/tag/v7.8.0)): Remove a dictionary entry by key with `removeKey`.
- **Chronicle.Kotlin** ([Release v7.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Kotlin/releases/tag/v7.8.0)): Set a property, or a dictionary entry, only while it has no value with `setOnce`, so the first event that produces a value wins.
- **Chronicle.Kotlin** ([Release v7.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Kotlin/releases/tag/v7.8.0)): Set a property from the identity of whoever caused an event with `toCausedBy`.
- **Chronicle.Kotlin** ([Release v7.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Kotlin/releases/tag/v7.8.0)): Keep children prepended or sorted by a child property, with `prepend`, `orderBy` and `orderByDescending` on fluent children and on `@ChildrenFrom` (#176)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): A dictionary entry in a projection can be keyed by a property of the event's content, for example `slots.$eventContent.slot`, alongside the existing event context and caused by keys. Set, set once, add, subtract, increment, decrement and count all accept such a key. An event whose key is missing or null skips the mapping, and a key path the event type does not have fails when the projection is registered (#5756)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): A dictionary key can be removed with the `$remove` operation, and every sink (MongoDB, SQL and in-memory) removes the entry instead of leaving it as null. Removing a key that does not exist does nothing (#5756)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): Set once assigns a property only when it has no value yet, on the root, on children, on nested objects and on dictionary entries, so a read model can keep who created an entry and when (#5757)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): Children can be ordered: new children can be prepended or kept sorted by a child property, ascending or descending. Equal values are ordered by the child's identifier, and MongoDB, SQL, in-memory and the projection engine all store the same order. Appending stays the default, and updating an existing child never moves it (#5758)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): The .NET client supports these operations in fluent projections (`SetOnce`, `RemoveKey`, dictionary keys from event content and children ordering) and in model-bound projections through the `SetFrom`, `SetFromContext` and `ChildrenFrom` attributes. They are also supported by `ReadModelScenario` and recognized by the projection analyzers (#5759)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.149.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.149.0)): Read a Neo4j server address and password from mounted files: `serverFile` on a named Neo4j sink, and `serverFile` and `passwordFile` on the `neo4j` section, with the same startup checks as the other file settings (#5771)
- **cli** ([Release v3.125.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.125.0)): `cratis build connect`, `run`, `once` and `status`: a builder host for Cratis Studio. It registers the machine with a short code, agrees the protocol version, claims slices, keeps the claim alive, runs the kit's verification commands (a failing command reports the slice as failed, never built), commits on the kit's branch and reports the outcome. Builds run with Claude Code natively, with Codex, Gemini CLI, Cursor, GitHub Copilot CLI or OpenCode over the Agent Client Protocol, or with any generator through `--exec`. Credentials stay in the operating system's credential store and are never written into prompts, orders, outcomes or logs. (#560)
- **cli** ([Release v3.125.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.125.0)): `cratis build init --kit <name|git url>` installs a build kit from Studio (checksum verified) or from Git (`#<branch, tag or commit>`, `--kit-folder`), scaffolds an empty project or prepares an existing codebase whose conventions the first build learns, writes the kit's instructions and skills where the chosen agent reads them, and records the installation in `.cratis/build/kit.json`. `cratis build kit validate` checks a kit for authors. (#561)
- **cli** ([Release v3.125.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.125.0)): `cratis build run --modeling --board <board> --agent <agent>` connects a local coding agent to a Studio board: it shows as connected, people hand it work by mentioning it in the board chat, and it answers there. It proposes changes for a person to confirm, or acts at once with `--unattended`; what it changes on the board is attributed to it. (#562)
- **cli** ([Release v3.125.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.125.0)): `cratis studio fetch --context <slice|feature|eventModel|domain> --id <id> --format markdown|json|yaml|toon [--out <path>]` fetches the context of part of a model for an AI agent or a pipeline, with the same Studio connection as `cratis build`. (#563)
- **AI** ([Release v7.147.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.147.0)): `cratis-check-handoff.mjs` checks a session handoff written with the `cratis-session-handoff` skill before the session ends: every template section is present, recorded commits and branches match the repository, and every running job has a complete watch row. Errors fail the check; warnings are advisory unless `--strict` is given (#1082)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): A `Production` is reconciled into Chronicle: a StatefulSet of the `cratis/chronicle` production image behind a headless Service whose port is named `chronicle` for `chronicle+srv://` discovery, a certificate from a cert-manager certificate authority, generated administrator and client credentials, and a connection Secret reported in `status.connectionSecretName` (part of #15)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): Replicas above one form one Orleans cluster through the database, and changing `version` rolls the StatefulSet (part of #15)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): `database.mode: Managed` runs MongoDB through the Percona operator, with pinned images, automatic upgrades off, TLS disabled, an 8Gi memory limit and settings for members, size, storage class and node placement (part of #16)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): `graph.mode` (`None`, `Managed` or `External`) and `graph.edition` (`Community` or `Enterprise`) give a Production a Neo4j for graph projections, with its password mounted from a Secret and a `GraphReady` condition (part of #98)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): SQL storage and named sinks are applied to the kernel from mounted Secrets, and a duplicate, unknown or unconfigured sink blocks the Production with a condition naming it (part of #99)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): An application on a Production reads its named MongoDB sinks: each sink's connection is mounted as a file and `Cratis__MongoDB__Sinks__<name>__ServerFile` names it, for the Cratis Arc release with Cratis/Arc#3770; SQL sinks, which Arc cannot read, are named by the warning condition `SinksReadable` (#99)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): `application.graph` binds the graph of the Production to Cratis.Arc.Neo4j through `Cratis__Neo4j__…`, with the password from a `secretKeyRef`, and a `GraphBound` condition (part of #98)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.9.0)): `set once <property> = <value>` assigns a projection property only while it has no value, for values such as who created an entry and when (#1220)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.9.0)): Dictionary entries keyed from the event's content with `<property>.$eventContent.<path>`, and from `$causedBy.subject`, `.name` or `.userName`, alongside the existing `$eventContext` keys
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.9.0)): `remove <property>.$eventContent.<path>` deletes a dictionary entry
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.9.0)): `insert first`, `insert last` and `sort by <property> [ascending|descending]` in a `children` block control where new children are placed
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.9.0)): Diagnostics `PLAY0956` to `PLAY0963` for misplaced or invalid dictionary keys, `remove` lines and children ordering, reported alike by the C# and TypeScript compilers and the editor
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.9.0)): Semantic model version 43 executes set once and ordered children in specifications
- **Arc** ([Release v22.182.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.182.0)): Read read models from Chronicle named MongoDB sinks: configure `Cratis:MongoDB:Sinks:<name>` with `Server` or `ServerFile`, and an injected `IMongoCollection<T>` for a read model marked `[Sink("<name>")]` (or Arc's `[ReadModelSink("<name>")]`) reads from that server, including observable queries (#3770)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.182.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.182.0)): Arc follows the Chronicle client's `DefaultSinkName` for the read models it registers.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.182.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.182.0)): Misconfigured MongoDB sinks stop startup with a message naming the sink; a read model naming an unconfigured sink fails with `UnknownMongoDBSink`.
- **AI** ([Release v7.146.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.146.0)): A `cratis-session-handoff` skill for long sessions that near their context limit, finish a milestone or must continue in a fresh session. It keeps a running `.ai-work/<task>/STATE.md` note and gives one handoff template with fixed sections: verified facts apart from hypotheses, the user's constraints verbatim, a table of running jobs and watches with what to do when each finishes, the next step and the files to read first. Resuming re-verifies mutable facts before acting on them. (#1081)

## Changed

- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.168.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.168.0)): Removing, renaming or retyping a property of a registered event type is still rejected with `EventTypeSchemaChanged` in every event store; breaking changes need a new generation. Chronicle's own event types are never evolved in place. (#5946)
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.24.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.24.1)): Requires `grpc` 1.0.6 or later.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.167.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.167.1)): The hosting documentation describes certificate renewal. The password and the encryption certificate are still read at startup.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.167.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.167.0)): The token cache file and its folder are readable and writable by the current user only, on Windows as well as Unix. The file is protected before the token is written and replaced atomically, and a cache file other users can read is ignored and rewritten. (#5598)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.167.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.167.0)): A cached token is never kept longer than the configured lifetime, even when the token itself expires later. (#5598)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.167.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.167.0)): The connection string documentation explains where the token cache lives and that, with SRV discovery, the selected target host receives the client credentials for the token request. (#5598)
- **Stage** ([Release v6.1.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v6.1.0)): The host and rendered applications omit raw event source ids from trace spans by default; opt in with `EventSourceIdTracing.Raw` or `EventSourceIdTracing.Hashed`. (#970)
- **Stage** ([Release v6.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v6.0.0)): Screenplay to Scene conversion runs through `Cratis.Scene.Screenplay` behind one entry, `StageSceneConversion`; Stage keeps the render plan, findings, routes and shipped packages
- **AI** ([Release v7.167.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.167.3)): Writing and review skills now check prose around every language tab, treat tab notes and security claims as claims to verify against source, search a series before changing a repeated fact, keep limits through length trims, and bound quick-review findings. (#459)
- **cli** ([Release v3.139.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.139.0)): `cratis build run --docker` picks the builder image for the target: `cratis/builder`, or `cratis/studio-axon-builder` with `--target axon`. It runs the newest version already pulled, or pulls the latest released version when none is. `--image` with a repository and no tag is resolved the same way.
- **cli** ([Release v3.139.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.139.0)): After a command, the CLI tells you when a newer version of a builder image you have is released, and `cratis update` pulls it, as it does for the Stage image.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.1)): Document the ownership rules for saved queries and folders in the Workbench: only the owner or an administrator can change or delete them, and sharing with Everyone controls visibility only (#5594)
- **Components** ([Release v4.42.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.42.3)): Restructured the chips field, password field, toaster, events view, chat topic list, filter panel and pivot viewer internals into smaller parts for maintainability; behavior is unchanged (part of #506)
- **Components** ([Release v4.42.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.42.2)): Restructured the command stepper, components provider, data table core, chips field and canvas internals into smaller parts for maintainability; behavior is unchanged (part of #506)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.8](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.8)): The Claude Code provider's MCP server, stream event parsing and chat client are restructured into smaller parts with the same behavior, with new specs for retries before initialization, stop reasons on non-text deltas and mixed assistant error content (part of #1080)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.7](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.7)): The Screenplay skills explain that a resolved, language-tagged handler can declare `may produce`, binds at ESM v35 with handler result contract v2 and returns an ordered selection of its declared events, and that PLAY0800 refuses only handlers with neither unconditional `produces` nor `may produce` (#1017)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.7](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.7)): The Screenplay skills cover nested collection sample rows, the hint for a misplaced `rule <predicate>`, and PLAY0166 as an error for an unresolved qualified `from` input of a `source events` capture
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.7](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.7)): The Stage rendering skill describes the construct-naming messages of STAGE-ESM-002, STAGE-ESM-014 and STAGE-ESM-015 and that an exact Int64 overflow in a whole-number projection product reports Unsupported (#904)
- **Components** ([Release v4.42.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.42.1)): Restructured the schema editor, type cell, column filter menu, chat sidebar and dialog internals into smaller parts for maintainability; behavior is unchanged (part of #506)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.3)): The new-repository intake rule treats a direct, named issue operation or an accepted scoped policy as enough authority, without an extra confirmation step; creating a repository or having tool access still grants no issue authority (part of #977)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.3)): The frontend developer agent hands browser checks to the browser operator with the URL, scope, permit and artifact folder, and reports the UI as unverified when no browser operator is available (part of #977)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.3)): Superseding a decision record is append-only: existing trailers and history stay as written and the new record id applies to new work (#979)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.3)): The GitHub Actions rule retries a bot push with a merge (`git pull --no-rebase`) instead of a rebase (#979)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.3)): Verification discipline separates the local Tier 1 gate from integration coverage, which may defer to CI only where CI enforces it; missing coverage fails closed and a stricter local policy wins (#983)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.3)): The Stage sandbox guidance documents the semantic engine as the default, with the legacy engine only on explicit request, and a verification check keeps the evaluations in line with the CLI and Stage behavior (#985)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.2)): The Orleans rule, the Copilot Orleans and framework instructions, and the `orleans` and `cratis-orleans-jobs` skills now use the `Cratis.Choreography` package names and `AddCratisChoreography` entry point from Cratis/Choreography 2.0.0, and mention the old `Cratis.Orleans` names only to explain the rename for upgraders (#1193)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.2)): The NuGet pin list used by the hooks now pins `Cratis.Choreography`, `Cratis.Choreography.Chronicle`, `Cratis.Choreography.Storage.MongoDB` and `Cratis.Choreography.Storage.Sql` 2.0.0 instead of the `Cratis.Orleans` packages; run `cratis ai update` to pick up the hook change (#1193)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.12.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.12.1)): `Cratis.Chronicle`, `Cratis.Chronicle.AspNetCore` and `Cratis.Chronicle.Testing` are updated to `19.164.4`
- **Arc** ([Release v23.12.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.12.1)): Chronicle 19.164.4: The per-reactor "Subscribing reactor" and "Reactor disconnected" log lines are now Debug instead of Information. Failures, timeouts and ungraceful disconnects keep their levels.
- **cli** ([Release v3.138.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.138.0)): Internal restructuring of the `new`, `run` and `extension verify` commands to improve maintainability, with no change in behavior. (part of #572)
- **cli** ([Release v3.137.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.137.1)): `cratis build run --docker` runs `cratis/builder` from Docker Hub by default, at the version of the CLI. Pass `--image` to run another image.
- **cli** ([Release v3.137.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.137.1)): The `cratis/builder` image is published to Docker Hub automatically for every CLI release.
- **cli** ([Release v3.137.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.137.0)): The builder speaks builder protocol versions 1 and 2.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.165.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.165.1)): "Client disconnected" and "Observer unsubscribed" are logged at Debug instead of Information. A client that disconnects ungracefully still logs its warning.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.165.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.165.0)): The projection editor recognizes graph declarations and no longer marks them as errors, so Preview and Save stay available.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.164.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.164.6)): "Observer deactivating", "Source event store became available" and "Event store subscription is ready to receive events" are logged at Debug instead of Information. Failures and warnings keep their levels.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.16.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.16.0)): `GrantedUsers` access mode no longer needs the same-kernel transport, and the host no longer refuses to start with it over `Http`.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.16.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.16.0)): The React and JavaScript packages now accept `@cratis/arc` and `@cratis/arc.react` 22.185.1 or later, and 23.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.15.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.15.0)): The `Understudy` resource is now reconciled, with status conditions, instead of schema only
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.15.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.15.0)): The operator chart grants CronJob and Job rights in managed namespaces and exposes a metrics port
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.14.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.14.0)): Changing `vault.mode` or `vault.managed.tier` on a Production that runs is refused, like the database and graph modes, because the keys stay where they are
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.14.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.14.0)): The operator's Role in each managed namespace also covers ConfigMaps, ServiceAccounts, Roles and RoleBindings (for the Vault helper) and deleting NetworkPolicies (for removed egress rules)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.4)): Screenplay skills now describe ESM v37 clock operands (`today`, `now`, `date of ...`), application and command calendars, declared context time zones and `given time zone` as executable in command conditions. The guidance also covers the Screenplay 5.5.0 minimum, where Stage refuses clock constructs per scope, that `every`/`at` clock triggers are still not realized, and why Studio refuses the typed-context revisions these constructs produce (#1022)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.13.0)): In `GrantedUsers` mode, `Availability` answers false for users who were not granted, the feedback button hides itself, and the note commands refuse them with the same message whether they were never granted or revoked.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.13.0)): `GrantedUsers` mode needs the same-kernel transport; the host refuses to start with it over the `Http` transport.
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.1)): The Screenplay read-surface, projection, specification and toolchain skills explain graph query `collect` with nested rows, ESM v54 and diagnostics `PLAY1144`–`PLAY1147`, and the corpus is pinned to the Screenplay 6.29.1 contract. (#1166)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.1)): The projection skills explain dictionary entries keyed from event content, set once and children ordering, so declarative projections replace reducers for these cases. (#1072)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.1)): The Screenplay toolchain and authoring skills explain `screenplay export`, `diff`, `rename` and persisted identities. (#1050)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.1)): The Screenplay skills explain admitting screens to the executable model with `--screens` and ESM v42. (#1071)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.1)): The Screenplay skills explain the OpenAPI and UI contract exports. (#1059)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.1)): The Screenplay skills explain the published ESM JSON Schemas and the per-slice change summary in `diff`. (#1051)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.1)): The Screenplay skills explain storylines and two-way value lineage with `trace-lineage` direction and `screenplay lineage`. (#1060)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.1)): The Screenplay skills explain development users (`user ... as <Persona>`) and how they relate to personas. (#1042)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.164.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.164.4)): The per-reactor "Subscribing reactor" and "Reactor disconnected" log lines are now Debug instead of Information. Failures, timeouts and ungraceful disconnects keep their levels.
- **AI** ([Release v7.163.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.163.0)): The Pi `cratis-mcp` extension no longer registers every selected MCP server's tools at session start. The agent starts a server on demand with the new `cratis_enable_mcp` tool, and its tools are callable from the next step. Set `mcpServers.<id>.eager` to `true` in `.cratis/ai.json` to register a server's tools at start-up as before. (#814)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.30.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.30.0)): VS Code offers only the C#-backed repairs the configured Screenplay server advertises. With an older server, the extension keeps offering the repairs that server supports instead of refusing to connect (part of #341)
- **AI** ([Release v7.161.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.161.0)): A model without the Vision capability, a provider client that cannot send images, or an unsupported image type refuses the completion with a clear reason instead of ignoring the image; a provider pool skips such a member and tries the next (#1148)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.4)): A new Screenplay release no longer turns the AI Publish run red: the scheduled Screenplay contract comparison reports drift as a warning, a run summary and the tracking issue, while releases keep checking the pinned contract (#1103)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.4)): Bumping the pinned Screenplay contract is one command, `yarn workspace @cratis/ai-verification run bump <version>`, which updates the contract, its version file and the compatibility table together; the procedure is in https://github.com/Cratis/AI/blob/main/Documentation/compatibility.md
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.4)): The Screenplay MCP tool reference no longer states a fixed tool count, and the preferred-syntax reference lists every style diagnostic in the pinned contract, including PLAY0955
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.2)): The Screenplay projection, read-surface and specification skills explain how to read a graph in queries and specifications, including `then query` steps and `given clock` to fix `$eventContext.occurred`. (#1039)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.2)): The Screenplay projection skill shows `foreach` in graph projection handlers and the diagnostics it reports. (#1040)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.2)): The Screenplay skills explain screen visuals, placing one slice in several places and `issue` notes on a model. (#1063)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.2)): The Screenplay skills explain `validate file`, `occurred` in scoped projections, host execution of rule, validation and reducer bodies, and admissible compilation. (#953)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.2)): The Screenplay projection, toolchain and model-review skills explain the `PLAY1142` warning for secret concepts copied into read models and how to fix it, and the corpus is pinned to the Screenplay 6.28.0 contract. (#1067)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.1)): The Screenplay read-surface skill explains how `for item` resolves on a screen with several `data` declarations: it acts on the one collection a `table` in scope shows. (#1117)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.1)): The Screenplay specification skills show how to write `given`, `when append` and `for case` specifications for events that no single command produces, with the identity taken from the read-model projections that consume them. (#1110)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.1)): The Screenplay skills explain how reaction bodies run through a host-supplied body execution provider, including which reactions qualify and what a body may return. (#959)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.1)): The Screenplay toolchain skill describes the opt-in unknown-preserving reader for consumers that keep unknown model additions without executing them, its admission range and its refusals. Requires Screenplay 4.256.0. (#957)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.1)): The Screenplay skills explain that read-model property declaration order is not preserved by default and how a C# host can opt in when binding a model. (#966)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.28.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.28.0)): Cratis Arc moves to 23.8.0, Chronicle to 19.162.0 and Screenplay to 6.29.0.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.28.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.28.0)): The renderer still refuses graph queries with STAGE-ESM-068; the message now says when the query collects.
- **cli** ([Release v3.136.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.136.0)): `--studio` accepts the builder address `https://<studio host>/builders/connect/<organization>` and takes the organization from it, instead of failing with `405 MethodNotAllowed`.
- **cli** ([Release v3.136.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.136.0)): The documented examples now use the hosted Studio's address rather than `https://cratis.studio`.
- **AI** ([Release v7.158.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.158.2)): The `cratis-documentation-writing` skill puts its completion checklist early and moves detailed writing guidance into a reference file with a pointer to when to read it (#973)
- **AI** ([Release v7.158.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.158.2)): The `cratis-writing-voice-and-cadence` skill keeps its stop conditions and core rules in the skill body and moves the longer voice guidance into a reference file (#973)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.12.0)): Showrunner now uses Chronicle 19.164.0, which hosts the federation client the page relies on.
- **AI** ([Release v7.158.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.158.1)): Screenplay skill bodies are reorganized into focused references so each skill stays within its size budget
- **Chronicle.Mcp** ([Release v2.3.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Mcp/releases/tag/v2.3.0)): Every tool result except `get_server_version` is wrapped in an `untrustedData` envelope (`source`, `tool`, `notice`, `content`). Hosts reading structured results must read `untrustedData.content` instead of the old top-level payload; the original JSON value is unchanged there.
- **Chronicle.Mcp** ([Release v2.3.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Mcp/releases/tag/v2.3.0)): Each text block starts with a notice and is enclosed in `CHRONICLE_UNTRUSTED_DATA` delimiters carrying a random per-call ID. The body is JSON-encoded so stored text cannot close the block; non-JSON text, including error messages, becomes a JSON string literal.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.11.0)): The message of an announcement is optional in authoring, validation and rendering
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.11.0)): `MarqueeLabels` has two new labels, `expand` and `collapse`; code that builds a complete `MarqueeLabels` object itself must add them
- **AI** ([Release v7.158.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.158.0)): `WorkerLaunchOutcome` has a new `ProviderAccessDenied` value, and `WorkerJob` has a `Provider` property that names the provider whose credential the worker receives (#998)
- **AI** ([Release v7.158.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.158.0)): Providers configured before this release stay open to everyone until a team member selection is set (#998)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.8.0)): `Cratis.Chronicle` packages move to 19.162.0.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.161.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.161.0)): Event type registration keeps migration validation and schema comparison in dedicated types, with unchanged behavior (#5889)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.65.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.65.0)): Bearer authentication fails closed when grant evidence is missing or inconsistent: DPoP-bound tokens, delegated or actor tokens, non-person subjects and client-credentials grants never receive an envelope, and nothing falls back to v1 or unsigned identity. Personal access tokens continue to be forwarded without an envelope (#344)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.65.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.65.0)): Cached introspection answers keep their original expiry, and the envelope's signing deadline never outlives it (#344)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.10.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.10.1)): `StartedTour.ByMe` is an observable query instead of a snapshot one, so the generated `ByMe.use()` hook in `@cratis/showrunner.cue` returns the result and a sorting setter, and no longer a perform-query function. `useCueTours` and `useTourNudge` in `@cratis/showrunner.cue.react` are unchanged for callers.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.159.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.159.0)): Projections resolve keys without array indexers and copy list state with less work per event. (part of #5824)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.7.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.7.0)): `Cratis.Arc.Availability` requires `Cratis.Choreography` 2.9.0 or later.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.9.0)): Cratis.Choreography updated to 2.9.0.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.8.0)): `AudienceGranted`, `AudienceWithdrawn` and `AudienceDecisionReceived` are generation 2 and carry `ClientId` (and `Kind` on the decisions). Generation 1 is kept and migrated, so an event published or stored before reads as the product-wide `System` holder
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.8.0)): `ProductGuestList`, `HeldAudience` and `UndeclaredAudienceGrant` carry `ClientId` and `Kind`
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.8.0)): Update products before granting an audience to a named system: a product that has not been updated ignores those decisions and never widens them to every system
- **AI** ([Release v7.157.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.157.0)): Worker jobs mount only the run's own memory key and the repositories it is authorized for, instead of the whole repository cache volume, on both Docker and Kubernetes; cache paths are verified on the host before launch (#1152)
- **AI** ([Release v7.157.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.157.0)): The secret and shell-write guards admit only reviewed command shapes and refuse everything else, including leading environment assignments outside a short list of display settings (#1076)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.5.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.5.1)): Authenticated callers without an identifying claim (name identifier, subject or name) can no longer manage subscriptions on server-sent-event observable query connections
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.157.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.157.0)): Rebuilding constraint indexes reads only the event types that constraint definitions use, so stored events of other types, including retired types without a registered schema, no longer stop the rebuild. (part of #5880)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.157.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.157.0)): Event type registration checks report a failing entry instead of aborting the whole check when one schema cannot be processed. (part of #5877)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.27.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.27.0)): Rendered applications reference Cratis.Choreography 2.8.0.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.7.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.7.0)): The undeclared grants report uses the audiences a product reported for its primary environment (`GuestListAuthoringOptions.PrimaryEnvironment`, default `Production`) before `DeclaredAudiences`, which stays as the fallback.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.7.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.7.0)): `Cratis.Choreography` is updated to 2.8.0.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.26.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.26.0)): Generated applications pin NuGet Arc 23.5.0 and Showrunner guest list 2.6.0; the npm Arc packages stay on 22.184.0, the line the Showrunner notes packages accept.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.26.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.26.0)): The generated backend scaffold gains an availability registration seam that does nothing when the model declares no availability.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.25.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.25.0)): Updated Cratis.Screenplay to 6.27.0, and the conformance matrix, the framework name catalog and the pinned render plan fingerprints with it
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.27.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.27.0)): Facts given before a specification's `given clock` take that instant as their occurrence time, so projections that read `occurred` run over them
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.27.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.27.0)): Facts a specification gives or produces without a caller are attributed to the `specification` audit identity
- **Stage** ([Release v5.24.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.24.0)): Stage now builds against Screenplay 6.26.0 and Choreography 2.7.0, and rendered applications pin Choreography 2.7.0. The scaffold adds `GeneratedProvideRegistration.cs` and registers it from `Program.cs`.
- **AI** ([Release v7.154.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.154.0)): The slice design, read surface, command surface, UI composition, model review, toolchain and streams skills point to the availability skill at the decisions where it matters
- **AI** ([Release v7.154.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.154.0)): The toolchain diagnostics fix guide, cheat sheet and language admission table cover PLAY1119-PLAY1141 and ESM v53
- **AI** ([Release v7.154.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.154.0)): The Screenplay contract pin moves to 6.26.0
- **Arc** ([Release v23.5.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.5.0)): Screenplay generation now builds on `Cratis.Screenplay` 6.26.0 and generation 0.21.2.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.26.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.26.0)): `provide` is admitted when its source is a literal, the host or a query; `provide` from a file or code block stays a preview.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.25.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.25.0)): Provided names and `$context.audiences` no longer raise `PLAY0153` as unknown context paths
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.25.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.25.0)): A model that uses `provide` in executable form reports `PLAY0268` until a later release admits it; it is a preview construct
- **Arc** ([Release v23.3.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.3.0)): Raises SharpCompress to 0.50.8, which `Cratis.Choreography` 2.7.0 requires.
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.9](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.9)): The screen composition and extension package skills now teach how a screen's `on <signal>` interaction runs: a handled signal is a Scene signal trigger bound by Stage, `signal.<value>` reads what the component raised, and it never fires an application trigger of the same name. They link the Stage and Scene pages by absolute URL. (part of #1121)
- **Scene** ([Release v5.4.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v5.4.1)): The task dialog a toolbar slice button opens no longer shows the slice's kind and model name (`State Change · PlaceOrder`) under its title. The person sees the button's title and the fields; the slice stays on the dialog's `data-scene-slice-dialog` attribute.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.23.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.23.0)): Stage now builds on Scene 5.4.0 for every Scene package, npm and NuGet, and on `@cratis/components` 4.40.1, which Scene 5.4.0 requires. Generated applications pin the new versions, so their plans have new hashes.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.23.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.23.0)): The Notifier sample screen handles its `dismissed` signal and the extensions documentation no longer describes signal binding as pending.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.22.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.22.0)): Stage builds against Screenplay 6.24.0, which the extension screens need. The renderer refuses the new ability invocation, workflow compute and state entry body members with `STAGE-ESM-016` until they run, and the conformance matrix tracks the v51 and v52 vectors. (part of #1118)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.22.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.22.0)): A model is compiled against the extension lock beside the model, not beside the directory Stage was started from. Starting Stage from another directory no longer reports `Extension '...' is not in screenplay.lock`.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.22.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.22.0)): A workflow action that invokes an ability is refused with `STAGE-LIFECYCLE-010` instead of failing while the host admits the model. Running ability invocations from reactions, workflow actions and state entries is not done yet. (part of #1118)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.64.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.64.1)): A valid DPoP proof that lacks the required scope now gets HTTP 403 `insufficient_scope` without reserving its proof identifier. Successful authorization still requires an atomic replay reservation. (#302)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.64.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.64.1)): The replay-store health check now gives up after a bounded wait and fails closed, rejecting the request, instead of holding requests while Redis is unresponsive. (#302)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.4)): The .NET client logs reactor registration, disconnection and reconnection at Information level, so a reactor that stops after a kernel restart shows up in the application logs (part of #5855)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.4)): Chronicle now uses Cratis Choreography 2.6.1, which keeps job-step state intact across restarts with SQL storage (part of #5855)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.4)): CI now posts a status comment when the nightly full test matrix on main goes red or recovers, and records the failed specs from a failing run so flaky lanes are visible without digging through logs (#5849)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.62.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.62.1)): Updated the shared Cratis AI guidance used for AI-assisted development in this repository
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.0.0)): Renamed the Usher contract event `IssueReopened` to `IssueReopenedByReporter`. Consumers must update their references to the new name.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.0.0)): Renamed the Usher contract event `WorkStarted` to `WorkStartedOnIssue`. Consumers must update their references to the new name.
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.8](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.8)): `DockerWorkerRuntime` starts the worker container first and delivers secrets through an exec into a root-owned tmpfs, then publishes a readiness marker that the worker entrypoint waits for before starting the agent. (#1047)
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.8](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.8)): Docker worker image references are preserved exactly as configured instead of being rewritten. (part of #1047)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.21.0)): Stage now depends on version 5.3.0 of every Scene package, up from 5.1.0, and on `@cratis/scene.extensions` 5.3.0. Scene 5.3.1 is not used until `@cratis/scene.extensions` 5.3.1 is published.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.21.0)): A screen's `on <signal>` interactions are not yet bound to a raised signal, because Scene 5.3.0 has no trigger kind for one. Raised signals reach `StageExtensionServicesProvider`'s `onSignal` and a `stage-extension-signal` event on the component's element.
- **Arc.Elixir** ([Release v1.1.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc.Elixir/releases/tag/v1.1.0)): Builds that add the analyzer can report new warnings after an upgrade that adds a rule. `ARCE0002` and `ARCE0005` default to errors; set a rule to `:warning` or `:off` under `config :cratis_arc, :analyzer` while you fix the findings.
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.7](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.7)): The environments skill covers extension settings, named secrets, activation and `cratis config --requires`.
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.7](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.7)): The toolchain skill explains the lock and restore diagnostics.
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.7](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.7)): The pinned Screenplay contract and compatibility table move to 6.24.0.
- **Templates** ([Release v1.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Templates/releases/tag/v1.9.0)): `Cratis.Templates.Extensions` is packed and published with the other template packages in each release. It is not part of `Cratis.Templates`.
- **cli** ([Release v3.134.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.134.0)): `cratis config set` always stores a key under `secrets.` as a secret, with or without `--secret`, and refuses a value given as an argument.
- **cli** ([Release v3.134.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.134.0)): `cratis config --missing` also lists the named secrets and extension settings a model needs.
- **cli** ([Release v3.134.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.134.0)): A secret passed to the Stage goes in a file only your user can read, passed with `--env-file` and deleted when the run ends; a secret value that spans several lines, or a registry host with a port, is refused by name.
- **cli** ([Release v3.134.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.134.0)): `cratis run` on a single `.play` file refuses a model that has extensions: run the folder that holds the model and the lock.
- **Stage.Axon** ([Release v0.11.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage.Axon/releases/tag/v0.11.1)): Stage.Axon is built with the latest C# language version instead of C# 14, in line with the other Cratis repositories on .NET 11. (#94)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.24.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.24.0)): A component placed as `<System>.<Component>` no longer needs the system's package in a `ui profile`: `PLAY0632` is not reported for a system that names an extension.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.24.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.24.0)): The vendored Invoicing extension contract changed (its `platform` line), so its digest in `screenplay.lock` and its folder under `extensions/` changed.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.19.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.19.0)): Receiving an inbound webhook no longer waits for a cold start: it is stored and acknowledged, and the backend starts when the stored request is delivered. `IExtensionEndpoints` gains `Knows`, which implementations that only resolve endpoints get for free.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.19.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.19.0)): `IExtensionPackages` gains `Systems`, which lists the systems of the model that name an extension. A custom implementation must add it.
- **Stage.Axon** ([Release v0.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage.Axon/releases/tag/v0.11.0)): Stage.Axon moves to Stage 5.11.0 / Screenplay 6.22.0 and admits semantic versions through V50; every new SDK member is classified (#90)
- **Stage.Axon** ([Release v0.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage.Axon/releases/tag/v0.11.0)): Screens, systems, agents, projection ordering and set-once, the new `Contains`/`StartsWith` conditions and caller-identity operands are reported as unsupported (warning, refused in strict mode) instead of being silently ignored (#90)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.1.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.1.0)): The Screenplay generator now depends on `Cratis.Screenplay` 6.23.0 (was 6.0.0). Generated models containing operations still bind to `PLAY0268` until Screenplay admits operations (Cratis/Screenplay#301) and ability invocations from operations (Cratis/Screenplay#1252); the generator accepts those messages as known admission limits.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.3)): The event store list (and its live view) shows only the event stores the caller is entitled to read when store entitlements are enforced; administrators and in-process callers still see every store (#5836)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.3)): Observers match events against large subscribed event-type sets with a set lookup, and projection saves skip future-save work when nothing is pending (#5822)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.3)): Pull request CI builds the spec binaries without analyzers and runs the analyzer build as a parallel required job, so the specs start sooner (#5837)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.3)): The SQL storage specs run each specification's setup once per class instead of once per fact, which shortens the SQL spec runs in pull request CI (#5832)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.18.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.18.0)): Stage requires `Cratis.Screenplay` 6.22.0, and the conformance matrix records its new ESM vector V50.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.18.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.18.0)): `/stage/extensions` is a tooling route: a published application does not serve it.
- **Scene** ([Release v5.3.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v5.3.1)): `@cratis/scene.components` and `@cratis/scene.blueprint.default` now accept Arc 23 (`@cratis/arc` and `@cratis/arc.react` below 24) and require `@cratis/components` 4.40.1 or later, the first Components release that accepts Arc 23 (#310)
- **cli** ([Release v3.131.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.131.0)): Chronicle client and integration image move to 19.156, with System event store commands run as administrator and refusals showing the administrator-login hint (#595)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.23.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.23.0)): Compute nodes, `approval ... via`, state entry bodies, operation-phase invocations and `$trigger` values are not executable yet: binding reports `PLAY0268` naming issue 1252.
- **Components** ([Release v4.40.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.40.1)): `@cratis/arc` and `@cratis/arc.react` peer ranges now accept Arc 23 (`>=20.3.1 <24`), so applications on Arc 23 install Components without peer dependency conflicts (#509)
- **cli** ([Release v3.130.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.130.0)): The CLI bundles Screenplay 6.22.0, which adds the extension contracts and the lock.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.22.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.22.0)): A system that names an `extension` can no longer declare abilities of its own (`PLAY1106`); the contract is the only source of its abilities.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.22.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.22.0)): Compiling a model whose system names an extension now needs `screenplay.lock` and the restored contract; run `screenplay restore` once and commit the lock (`PLAY1107`, `PLAY1108`).
- **Stage** ([Release v5.17.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.17.1)): Rendered C# places preprocessor directives at column 0, so generated applications pass `dotnet format whitespace --verify-no-changes` without a format pass (#1084)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.17.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.17.1)): Restructured specification and run admission for maintainability, with no behavior change (part of #1051)
- **cli** ([Release v3.129.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.129.0)): Chronicle applications and users commands (and the Workbench lists) work with an administrator login and show a `cratis chronicle login` hint when Chronicle refuses them (#557)
- **cli** ([Release v3.129.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.129.0)): Chronicle commands now exit with the authentication error code, instead of the server error code, when the server rejects the credentials or the stored login token, and say whether to check the credentials or run `cratis chronicle login` again
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.61.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.61.0)): Requests to a bearer route over plain HTTP are refused before any credential is read and are never forwarded. `LocalDevelopment` and loopback requests are exempt. Deployments that terminate TLS in front of AuthProxy must trust that proxy (#323).
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.61.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.61.0)): Bearer routes forward `X-Forwarded-Proto` with the effective scheme, replacing any client-supplied value (#324).
- **Arc** ([Release v23.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.0.0)): Read protection is on by default for commands that inject read models; set `<CratisReadProtection>Off</CratisReadProtection>` or adopt `On` through `Measure`. See the [upgrade guide](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/blob/main/Documentation/upgrading/read-protection-default.md) (#3255)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.0.0)): Strict key coverage is the default: `ProtectedReadModelKeyOptions.Strict` is `true`, and `ReadProtection:RequireGeneratedMetadata` is `true` unless set to `false` on a runtime with dynamic code support. Enable the source generator or register key accessors, or opt out explicitly. See the [upgrade guide](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/blob/main/Documentation/upgrading/read-protection-default.md#strict-key-coverage-is-the-default) (#3393)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.0.0)): The MCP endpoint is gated by default after routing when no selection is set, and `McpRequiresGatedEndpoints` is removed. See the [MCP upgrade guide](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/blob/main/Documentation/upgrading/mcp-gated-by-default.md) (#3777)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.0.0)): `MapCratisArcMcp()` is now `MapArcMcp()` with an obsolete forwarder, string `ExposeQuery` is replaced by `ExposeQuery<TReadModel>`, and `McpToolCeiling` is now `McpToolCatalog` with no forwarder. See the [MCP upgrade guide](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/blob/main/Documentation/upgrading/mcp-gated-by-default.md) (#3775)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.0.0)): The MCP authorization vectors are pinned to version 2.0.0
- **cli** ([Release v3.128.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.128.1)): The CLI uses Cratis.Fundamentals 7.34.0. (#591)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.16.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.16.0)): Reactions started by a raised trigger run as the actor `extension:<System>`, the extension that raised it, instead of the anonymous system actor.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.16.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.16.0)): `POST` requests to an extension endpoint are answered `202` with an empty body, not with the extension's answer. `GET`, `HEAD` and `OPTIONS` are still answered with what the extension answers.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.16.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.16.0)): A path an extension does not declare is rejected at delivery, rather than answered `404`, until the extension has run once.
- **Stage.Axon** ([Release v0.10.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage.Axon/releases/tag/v0.10.0)): `Cratis.Stage.Rendering.Axon` targets `net10.0` and `net11.0`; building the repository requires the .NET 11 SDK (11.0.100 or a later feature band, no previews) (#87)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.21.0)): Renaming a secret updates its declaration and every `from secret` mapping.
- **Arc.TypeScript** ([Release v1.0.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc.TypeScript/releases/tag/v1.0.1)): Simplified the PostgreSQL observation manager and the proxy generator's source type resolver for maintainability, with new characterization specs and no behavior or public API change (part of #206)
- **Stage.Axon** ([Release v0.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage.Axon/releases/tag/v0.9.0)): Stage.Axon now builds against Cratis.Stage.Contracts 5.0.0 and Cratis.Screenplay 6.0.0, with the vendored corpus migrated to Screenplay 6 syntax (#89)
- **Stage.Axon** ([Release v0.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage.Axon/releases/tag/v0.9.0)): The renderer refuses Screenplay 6 constructs it cannot render (state machines, workflows, graph, clock and calendar operands, one-of, possible events, date-only operands, application time zone and context members) with `STAGE-AXON-UNSUPPORTED-SEMANTICS`: a warning by default, an error in strict mode (#89)
- **Stage.Axon** ([Release v0.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage.Axon/releases/tag/v0.9.0)): Generated TypeScript client files now carry the generated-file header, which Stage 5 owns (#89)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.15.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.15.0)): A backend answers the invocations of its own system without the persona or pol
- **Stage** ([Release v5.14.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.14.0)): Stage takes Chronicle 19.155.1, which is the first release with set-once, Cratis.Fundamentals 7.34.0 and Cratis.Choreography 2.3.1. Generated applications pin the new versions.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.14.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.14.0)): The ESM feature matrix lists versions 43 to 49 as audited.
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.6](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.6)): Workflow, state machine and specification guidance writes `invokes` and `starts` instead of the deprecated `sends`, `entering send` and `entering start`.
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.6](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.6)): The pinned Screenplay contract and compatibility table move to 6.20.1.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.1)): Restructured Vault key-store migration and secret stores into smaller units with no behavior change (#5768)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.1)): Simplified mutation validation and the SQL mutation registry with no behavior change (#5664)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.13.0)): A workflow action that invokes a command now leaves through `refused` when the command was handed on and rejected, and through `failed` only when the invocation itself broke. A refusal that no outlet routes still fails the node. The rendered action performers behave the same way. (part of #1251)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.13.0)): Upgraded to Cratis.Choreography 2.5.0, Cratis.Chronicle 19.148.0 (the version Choreography 2.5.0 requires) and Cratis.Screenplay 6.19.0.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.13.0)): The sandbox host no longer registers a no-op Chronicle telemetry callback, because Chronicle now binds its telemetry configuration itself.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.13.0)): A `once` ability is refused while the host keeps its grain storage in memory, because a restart would lose the record that stops a repeat. Set `Stage:Orleans:Storage` to `MongoDB`, or set `Stage:Invocations:AllowVolatileOnce` to `true` in a sandbox.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.20.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.20.1)): Builds with the latest C# language version and suggests collection-expression and labeled `break`/`continue` forms in the IDE; `Screenplay.Conformance` still compiles with C# 12 because its sources ship to consumers. (#1258)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.20.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.20.1)): Simplified the workspace timeline repair and content-proof code without changing its behavior, and pinned guarded-action diagnostic order and timeline repair outcomes with new specifications. (part of #1238)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.20.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.20.1)): The Screenplay benchmarks executable targets .NET 11 only. (#1259)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.20.0)): A system that declares an ability now binds; operations and systems without abilities still report `PLAY0268`.
- **Prompter** ([Release v0.14.22](https://github.com/Cratis/Prompter/releases/tag/v0.14.22)): Internal refactors of Discord answer splitting, forum answering, evaluation console output and groundedness scoring, with no change in behavior. (part of #151)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.0)): The client subscription keeper no longer calls every connected reactor and reducer every 2 seconds. It checks subscriptions when a silo leaves the cluster, plus a jittered safety net, which cuts its grain calls on an idle kernel about twentyfold. The restore bounds for each kind of lost subscription are documented (#5816)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.0)): Observer metric instruments are created the first time an observer records them, and the instruments of a scope share their tags, which saves about 3 KiB for each observer activation that records no metrics. Metric names, units and tags are unchanged (#5815)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.0)): The container profile guidance now follows the measurements: keep the image's default Server GC with DATAS, use Workstation GC only when memory rather than CPU limits a container with several CPUs, and do not turn DATAS off
- **Prompter** ([Release v0.14.21](https://github.com/Cratis/Prompter/releases/tag/v0.14.21)): Internal refactors of section merging, issue events, endpoints, Discord answers and hand-offs, README ingestion, task chat policy, retrieval passages, evaluation output and deployment definitions, with no change in behavior. (part of #151)
- **Templates** ([Release v1.8.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Templates/releases/tag/v1.8.5)): Projects generated from the Cratis, Cratis Aspire, Chronicle console and Chronicle web templates now compile with the latest C# language version for the SDK in use
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.19.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.19.0)): Write `action <Node> invokes <Command>`, `entering invokes <Command>`, `entering starts <Workflow>` and `then invokes <Command>`. The old `sends`, `entering send`, `entering start` and `then sends` forms still compile but raise the deprecation warning `PLAY0397`, with a typed repair that migrates them. Printing a document writes the new forms.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.19.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.19.0)): The C# and TypeScript compilers, the reference evaluator, Monaco completions and hover, the VS Code grammar, the samples and the documentation use the new forms. ESM bytes do not change for the verb.
- **cli** ([Release v3.128.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.128.0)): New Cratis applications rendered with `cratis render` target .NET 11; existing applications keep their recorded profile and render unchanged (#579)
- **cli** ([Release v3.128.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.128.0)): Rendering uses Stage 5.11.0 and Screenplay 6.18.0 (#579)
- **cli** ([Release v3.128.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.128.0)): `cratis render` admits the Screenplay model versions Stage 5.11.0 supports, including the v34 models Screenplay 6 produces (#579)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.12.0)): Generated shared policy runtime files now contain the identifier helper, so their bytes change for every application that has policies.
- **Screenplay.Generation** ([Release v0.21.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.Generation/releases/tag/v0.21.2)): Ordinal-compared sets and dictionaries and empty working lists in generation and .NET source placement use C# 15 collection expressions (#95)
- **Screenplay.CritterStack** ([Release v0.27.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.CritterStack/releases/tag/v0.27.2)): Marten projection discovery builds its symbol set with a C# 15 collection expression argument (#111)
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.5](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.5)): Use the C# `field` keyword and collection expressions in Cratis.AI internals; no behavior or API change (#1135)
- **Prompter** ([Release v0.14.20](https://github.com/Cratis/Prompter/releases/tag/v0.14.20)): Internal refactors of markdown chunking, admission, usage pricing, documentation metadata and mentions handling, with no change in behavior. (part of #151)
- **Choreography** ([Release v2.4.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Choreography/releases/tag/v2.4.2)): Lookup sets, the grain contract manifest and the ForEach branch list are built with C# 15 collection expression arguments, and the branch list is pre-sized to the item count (#209)
- **cli** ([Release v3.127.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.127.1)): Internal collections and the workbench refresh loop now use C# 15 collection expression arguments and `System.Threading.Lock`, with no change in behavior (#584)
