- **Synopsis** ([Release v1.2.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Synopsis/releases/tag/v1.2.3)): Restructured the C#, TypeScript, Gherkin and Screenplay specification parsers, the path classifier and the `synopsis` command-line entry point into smaller, focused types. Discovery results, generated reports, command-line output and exit codes are unchanged. (#28)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.155.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.155.1)): Collection-expression and related modern C# simplifications are now reported as suggestions instead of being silenced, and kernel hot paths use collection expressions, `Lock` and single enumeration (#5811)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.155.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.155.1)): Simplified projection property mapping, event value resolution, future resolution and SQL JSON column handling internals (part of #5767)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.155.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.155.1)): Restructured observer fast-forward, webhook delivery, projection event context, read model serialization, client artifact discovery, read model scenarios, OAuth client and certificate loading into smaller units with no behavior change (part of #5767)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.155.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.155.1)): Restructured constraint-index replay, event-sequence constraint-index handling and unique-constraint storage into smaller units with no behavior change (part of #5031)
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.15.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.15.0)): Reactor and reducer observation streams now reconnect with a jittered wait that grows while a stream keeps ending without having worked (2 seconds doubling up to a minute, starting over once a stream delivered or stayed open for three minutes), instead of retrying on a fixed interval, so clients no longer return as one herd during a kernel rollout. The defaults match the .NET client (part of Cratis/Chronicle#5817)
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.15.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.15.0)): When the kernel no longer knows the client's session, for example after a kernel restart, the client drops the session once and reconnects with a new connection id, and its reactors and reducers register again, instead of retrying the stream against the lost session. Reports about an earlier session are ignored, and session reconnects also back off with jitter (part of Cratis/Chronicle#5817)
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.15.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.15.0)): Errors thrown by your own reactor or reducer handlers no longer count as a lost stream or session
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.23.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.23.0)): Reactor and reducer observation streams now reconnect with a jittered wait that grows while a stream keeps ending without having worked (2 seconds doubling up to a minute, starting over once a stream delivered or stayed open for three minutes), instead of retrying on a fixed interval, so clients no longer return as one herd during a kernel rollout. The defaults match the .NET client (part of Cratis/Chronicle#5817)
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.23.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.23.0)): When the kernel no longer knows the client's session, for example after a kernel restart, the client drops the session once and reconnects with a new connection id, and its reactors and reducers register again, instead of retrying the stream against the lost session. Reports about an earlier session are ignored, and session reconnects also back off with jitter (part of Cratis/Chronicle#5817)
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.23.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.23.0)): Errors thrown by your own reactor or reducer handlers no longer count as a lost stream or session
- **Chronicle.TypeScript** ([Release v6.63.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.TypeScript/releases/tag/v6.63.0)): Reactor and reducer observation streams now reconnect with a jittered wait that grows while a stream keeps ending without having worked (2 seconds doubling up to a minute, starting over once a stream delivered or stayed open for three minutes), instead of retrying on a fixed interval, so clients no longer return as one herd during a kernel rollout. The defaults match the .NET client (part of Cratis/Chronicle#5817)
- **Chronicle.TypeScript** ([Release v6.63.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.TypeScript/releases/tag/v6.63.0)): When the kernel no longer knows the client's session, for example after a kernel restart, the client drops the session once and reconnects with a new connection id, and its reactors and reducers register again, instead of retrying the stream against the lost session. Reports about an earlier session are ignored, and session reconnects also back off with jitter (part of Cratis/Chronicle#5817)
- **Chronicle.TypeScript** ([Release v6.63.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.TypeScript/releases/tag/v6.63.0)): Errors thrown by your own reactor or reducer handlers no longer count as a lost stream or session
- **Chronicle.Kotlin** ([Release v7.10.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Kotlin/releases/tag/v7.10.0)): Reactor and reducer observation streams now reconnect with a jittered wait that grows while a stream keeps ending without having worked (2 seconds doubling up to a minute, starting over once a stream delivered or stayed open for three minutes), instead of retrying on a fixed interval, so clients no longer return as one herd during a kernel rollout. The defaults match the .NET client (part of Cratis/Chronicle#5817)
- **Chronicle.Kotlin** ([Release v7.10.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Kotlin/releases/tag/v7.10.0)): When the kernel no longer knows the client's session, for example after a kernel restart, the client drops the session once and reconnects with a new connection id, and its reactors and reducers register again, instead of retrying the stream against the lost session. Reports about an earlier session are ignored, and session reconnects also back off with jitter (part of Cratis/Chronicle#5817)
- **Chronicle.Kotlin** ([Release v7.10.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Kotlin/releases/tag/v7.10.0)): Errors thrown by your own reactor or reducer handlers no longer count as a lost stream or session
- **Synopsis** ([Release v1.2.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Synopsis/releases/tag/v1.2.2)): The C# specification parser uses C# 15 collection expression arguments for its case-insensitive lookup sets and reuses one empty attribute set instead of allocating one per scenario (#33)
- **Architecture** ([Release v1.0.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Architecture/releases/tag/v1.0.4)): The analyzers are built with the .NET 11 SDK, and the unused-interface rule uses C# 15 collection expression arguments for its symbol sets (#55)
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.4)): Rules, agents, skills and the shipped quality gate now name `-p:CratisProxiesSkip=true` (Arc 22.185.1 or later) as the way to skip TypeScript proxy generation. Command lines that run against any Arc version pass it together with the empty `-p:CratisProxiesOutputPath=`, which still turns the generator off on older Arc versions. Run `cratis ai update` to pick up the new quality gate (#1132)
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.4)): The `cratis-arc-configuration` evaluation recognizes `DisableProxyGenerator` as Arc's alias for `CratisProxiesSkip` (#1132)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.186.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.186.0)): Refactored internal command, query, proxy-generation and authorization code for maintainability with no behavior change. (part of #3767)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.186.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.186.0)): Updated the specs to Cratis.Specifications 5.3.0. (#3795)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.186.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.186.0)): Reading query string arguments no longer allocates a new set of reserved keys on every request. (#3798)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.21.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.21.3)): Updated Cratis Arc to 22.185.1, Cratis Fundamentals to 7.34.0 and Cratis Chronicle to 19.155.0, including the `@cratis/arc`, `@cratis/arc.react` and `@cratis/fundamentals` frontend packages.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.21.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.21.3)): Builds that do not need TypeScript proxies now skip proxy generation entirely, so only the frontend build generates proxies and checks them for drift.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.9.0)): Stage targets .NET 11 as well as .NET 10: the packages ship both, and the Stage Host and SpecRunner images run on .NET 11 (#1047)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.9.0)): Stage packages embed their Screenplay documents again.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.9.0)): Stage builds on Screenplay 6.18.0.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.9.0)): Documentation page descriptions are shorter, so search results show them in full.
- **cli** ([Release v3.127.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.127.0)): The `cratis` tool now runs on .NET 11, and the published `Cratis.Environments` library targets .NET 10 and .NET 11. (#570)
- **cli** ([Release v3.127.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.127.0)): `cratis run` lets Ante trust the identity that AuthProxy forwards, and keeps Ante reachable only through AuthProxy. (#571)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.155.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.155.0)): The Chronicle Kernel server and its Docker images run on .NET 11 (part of #5763)
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.3)): Pull request and verify CI builds no longer generate Arc TypeScript proxies, and the proxy drift gate is removed. The npm release build still regenerates them, and the docs say how to refresh the committed proxies locally with `dotnet build Source/Cratis.AI/Cratis.AI.csproj`. (#1130)
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.3)): The C# rule now covers the .NET 11 SDK analyzers IDE0370, IDE0001 and IDE0002: fix the findings, keep warning severity, and the narrow multi-target exception for IDE0370. Run `cratis ai update` to pick it up. (#1129)
- **Eventmodelers-Build-Kit-CSharp** ([Release v0.1.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Eventmodelers-Build-Kit-CSharp/releases/tag/v0.1.1)): The starter application and test projects use the latest C# version. (#41)
- **Eventmodelers-Build-Kit-CSharp** ([Release v0.1.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Eventmodelers-Build-Kit-CSharp/releases/tag/v0.1.1)): The repository pins the .NET SDK to 11.0.100, matching the generated projects. (part of #41)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.185.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.185.0)): Builds with the .NET 11 GA SDK and Microsoft.Extensions 11.0.0 packages on net11.0; libraries keep net10.0 support.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.185.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.185.0)): Updated Cratis.Fundamentals to 7.34.0.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.185.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.185.0)): The net11.0 target keeps the Entity Framework Core stack on 10.0.x until the PostgreSQL provider for EF Core 11 is published (#3794).
- **Screenplay.CritterStack** ([Release v0.27.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.CritterStack/releases/tag/v0.27.1)): Screenplay.CritterStack builds with the newest C# language version of the SDK in use instead of a fixed C# 14 pin. (#108)
- **Screenplay.Generation** ([Release v0.21.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.Generation/releases/tag/v0.21.1)): Screenplay.Generation builds with the newest C# language version of the SDK in use instead of a fixed C# 14 pin. (#92)
- **Ante** ([Release v1.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.13.0)): Ante and its legacy consumer now build on .NET 11 (SDK 11.0.100); the contracts packages target net10.0 and net11.0
- **Ante** ([Release v1.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.13.0)): Container images use the .NET 11 ASP.NET runtime
- **Ante** ([Release v1.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.13.0)): CI no longer generates Arc proxies; the checked-in frontend proxies are used (#234)
- **Ante** ([Release v1.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.13.0)): Improved the structure of the application host for maintainability (#229)
- **Prologue** ([Release v3.2.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Prologue/releases/tag/v3.2.1)): Prologue builds with the newest C# language version of the SDK in use instead of a fixed C# 14 pin. (#95)
- **Templates** ([Release v1.8.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Templates/releases/tag/v1.8.4)): The `cratis`, `cratis-aspire`, `cratis-chronicle-console` and `cratis-chronicle-web` templates now target .NET 11 (SDK 11.0.100)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.154.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.154.1)): The production image `cratis/chronicle` is based on `mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/runtime-deps:10.0-noble-chiseled-extra` instead of `aspnet:10.0-noble-chiseled-extra`, which makes it about 170 MB smaller. The kernel was already self-contained, so it behaves the same, but an image built `FROM cratis/chronicle` no longer contains a shared .NET runtime or the `dotnet` host
- **Synopsis** ([Release v1.2.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Synopsis/releases/tag/v1.2.1)): Synopsis builds with the newest C# language version of the SDK in use instead of a fixed C# 15 pin. (#31)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.21.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.21.2)): Restructured support access, conversation access, issue state, forwarded request and session callback verification code into smaller named steps, keeping every check and its order unchanged (part of #110)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.21.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.21.2)): Restructured on-behalf token verification and stage production and spec validation into smaller named steps, keeping the same checks, order and problem messages (part of #110)
- **Montage** ([Release v0.1.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Montage/releases/tag/v0.1.1)): Montage builds with the latest C# language version, so C# 15 features are available on .NET 11. (#14)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.60.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.60.4)): Simplified tenant authentication state, user token storage, client credentials token protection, client principal building, link subject exchange, invite callback completion, identity endpoint calls and service routing validation into smaller, typed steps (part of #298)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.60.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.60.4)): Removed duplicated configuration code behind the Aspire `AuthProxyExtensions` methods; their public signatures are unchanged (part of #298)
- **Eventmodelers-Build-Kit-CSharp** ([Release v0.1.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Eventmodelers-Build-Kit-CSharp/releases/tag/v0.1.0)): The C# starter targets .NET 11 (`net11.0`) and pins the .NET 11 SDK 11.0.100 in its `global.json`; install the .NET 11 SDK to build and run a generated app. (#39)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.154.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.154.0)): `cratis.chronicle.observer.successful_observations` now also counts events delivered by catch-up, replay and retry jobs, tagged with `cratis.replay`, so dashboards show the throughput of replays and catch-ups as well as live delivery
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.154.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.154.0)): A required startup step whose calls time out against this silo or an active sibling that is busy catching up now waits with backoff, without counting attempts and while the kernel stays unready, instead of terminating the process after its retries. The wait is bounded by `Startup:BusySiloWaitLimit`, two hours by default (#5676)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.154.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.154.0)): The store entitlements notice is given once the silo is active instead of before the kernel binds its ports, so a kernel enforcing store entitlements binds and reports liveness while it joins a cluster with a backlog (#4040)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.60.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.60.3)): Simplified invitation staging, identity account binding and reverse proxy route and cluster configuration without changing their behavior (part of #298)
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.0)): Worker git commands run non-interactively, so a missing credential or prompt fails fast instead of hanging the worker (#1069)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.21.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.21.1)): The code now builds with the latest C# language version and uses C# 15 collection expression arguments where they replace constructor calls, with no change in behavior (#108)
- **Choreography** ([Release v2.4.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Choreography/releases/tag/v2.4.0)): The grain compatibility tool runs on .NET 11 (#197)
- **Choreography** ([Release v2.4.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Choreography/releases/tag/v2.4.0)): The job engine, workflow runs, operations, serializers and job step storage are internally simplified, with no change in behavior (part of #198)
- **Choreography** ([Release v2.4.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Choreography/releases/tag/v2.4.0)): Shortened two collaboration page search descriptions
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.60.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.60.2)): Simplified invitation completion, tenant selection, reverse proxy anonymous cluster resolution, bearer route profile selection, canonical identity binding, user access token refresh and canonical identity configuration validation without changing their behavior (part of #298)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.18.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.18.0)): An action declared `for item` on a screen with several `data` blocks now binds to the one collection that a `table` in scope shows, instead of failing with PLAY0346. When the subject is still ambiguous, PLAY0346 names the candidate data. (#1209)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.18.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.18.0)): Targets .NET 10 and .NET 11; .NET 8 and .NET 9, which reach end of support on 10 November 2026, are no longer targeted. The `cratis` tool and the container image run on .NET 11. (#1224)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.18.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.18.0)): Decimal values in the executable semantic model are lowered identically on .NET 10 and .NET 11, so models produce the same results on both runtimes. (#1224)
- **AI** ([Release v7.152.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.152.2)): The Claude Code provider, the provider-aware language model and the pooled chat client are restructured into smaller parts with the same behavior, with new specs for MCP request validation and structured output (part of #1080)
- **AI** ([Release v7.152.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.152.2)): The `score-fidelity.mjs` script in the Screenplay reverse-engineering skill is split into smaller helpers with identical output (part of #1080)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.60.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.60.1)): Tenant resolution, identity details resolution, canonical identity resolution and tenancy handling are restructured into smaller, simpler steps with the same behavior (part of #298)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.60.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.60.0)): The AuthProxy service and its container image now run on .NET 11, using the `mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/aspnet:11.0` runtime image (#291)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.60.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.60.0)): The Aspire hosting integration package now targets both .NET 10 and .NET 11
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.60.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.60.0)): ASP.NET Core and runtime package dependencies move to 11.0.0 on .NET 11; the .NET 10 target keeps the 10.0 packages
- **AI** ([Release v7.152.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.152.1)): The C# rules now cover C# 15 and .NET 11: keep `LangVersion` at `latest`, put new .NET 11 runtime APIs in multi-targeted libraries behind `#if NET11_0_OR_GREATER` with a .NET 10 fallback, use collection expression arguments (`[with(capacity: n), .. items]`) and labeled `break`/`continue`, and keep IDE style rules at `suggestion` severity.
- **AI** ([Release v7.152.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.152.1)): The C# rules now say not to adopt `union` types, runtime-async or the memory-safety preview, and not to add `closed` to existing public types.
- **AI** ([Release v7.152.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.152.1)): The C# code-quality rules now prefer a sealed record hierarchy with an exhaustive `switch` for a closed set of outcomes, with `closed` allowed only on new types in .NET 11 code.
- **Prompter** ([Release v0.14.19](https://github.com/Cratis/Prompter/releases/tag/v0.14.19)): Prompter's code style settings now suggest C# 15 collection expressions and labeled loop jumps, and two comparer-backed collections use the new collection-expression arguments, with no change in behavior.
- **Prompter** ([Release v0.14.18](https://github.com/Cratis/Prompter/releases/tag/v0.14.18)): Prompter now builds with the .NET 11 SDK (11.0.100) and runs on the .NET 11 runtime; the build and runtime container images move to the digest-pinned .NET 11 GA images. (#148)
- **AI** ([Release v7.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.152.0)): The Cratis.AI package targets `net10.0` and `net11.0`, so .NET 11 applications get a native .NET 11 build while .NET 10 applications keep working unchanged.
- **AI** ([Release v7.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.152.0)): The repository builds with the .NET 11 GA SDK (11.0.100).
- **AI** ([Release v7.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.152.0)): The agent harness base image is built on the .NET 11 GA SDK image and still includes the .NET 10 runtimes the Cratis CLI and the Roslyn language server need, so both tools keep starting inside the image.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.21.0)): The Showrunner packages target `net10.0` and `net11.0`, so .NET 11 applications get a native build while .NET 10 applications keep working (#106)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.21.0)): Stage Manager targets `net11.0` and its image uses the `mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/runtime:11.0` base (#106)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.21.0)): The repository builds with the .NET 11 SDK (`11.0.100`) (#106)
- **Montage** ([Release v0.1.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Montage/releases/tag/v0.1.0)): Montage runs on .NET 11: it targets `net11.0`, builds with the .NET 11 SDK, ships on the .NET 11 container images and uses Arc 22.184.0, Chronicle 19.153.2 and Choreography 2.3.1. The local Chronicle kernel image is 19.153.2. (#12)
- **Chronicle.Mcp** ([Release v2.2.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Mcp/releases/tag/v2.2.0)): The MCP server targets .NET 11 and its container image is based on the .NET 11 runtime image on Ubuntu 26.04 (`mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/runtime:11.0-resolute`) (#66)
- **Chronicle.Mcp** ([Release v2.2.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Mcp/releases/tag/v2.2.0)): Building from source requires the .NET 11 SDK (11.0.100 or later) (#66)
- **Prologue** ([Release v3.2.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Prologue/releases/tag/v3.2.0)): Packages target .NET 10 and .NET 11, and the Receiver, Interpreter and Extractor applications and their container images run on .NET 11. (#92)
- **Prologue** ([Release v3.2.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Prologue/releases/tag/v3.2.0)): Agent responses are parsed with fewer allocations.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.8.0)): Takes Screenplay 6.17.0.
- **Screenplay.Generation** ([Release v0.21.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.Generation/releases/tag/v0.21.0)): Targets .NET 10 and .NET 11. .NET 8 and .NET 9, which reach end of support on 10 November 2026, are no longer targeted. (#89)
- **Fundamentals** ([Release v7.34.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Fundamentals/releases/tag/v7.34.0)): Cratis.Fundamentals and Cratis.OpenTelemetry now target .NET 10 and .NET 11. .NET 8 and .NET 9, which reach end of support on 10 November 2026, are no longer targeted. (#1267)
- **Screenplay.CritterStack** ([Release v0.27.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.CritterStack/releases/tag/v0.27.0)): Targets .NET 10 and .NET 11. .NET 8 and .NET 9, which reach end of support on 10 November 2026, are no longer targeted. (#105)
- **Scene** ([Release v5.2.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v5.2.0)): Targets .NET 10 and .NET 11. .NET 8 and .NET 9, which reach end of support on 10 November 2026, are no longer targeted. (#293)
- **Specifications** ([Release v5.3.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Specifications/releases/tag/v5.3.0)): Targets .NET 10 and .NET 11. `Cratis.Specifications`, `Cratis.Specifications.XUnit`, `Cratis.Specifications.NUnit` and the other packages now ship `net10.0` and `net11.0` builds; .NET 8 and 9 remain unsupported (they reached end of support on 10 November 2026). (#110)
- **Specifications** ([Release v5.3.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Specifications/releases/tag/v5.3.0)): Building the repository requires the .NET 11 SDK. (#110)
- **Synopsis** ([Release v1.2.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Synopsis/releases/tag/v1.2.0)): Cratis.Synopsis now targets .NET 11 in addition to .NET 10. (#29)
- **Synopsis** ([Release v1.2.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Synopsis/releases/tag/v1.2.0)): The `synopsis` .NET tool now runs on .NET 11; install the .NET 11 runtime to use it.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.7.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.7.0)): Takes Screenplay 6.16.0.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.153.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.153.1)): A failure that repeats with the same cause during a cold start is logged in full once per interval, followed by a summary of how many more occurred, instead of one stack trace per occurrence. This applies to every log provider, including suppressed unobserved task exceptions, and a failed event store subscription refresh is no longer logged twice (#5678)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.153.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.153.1)): An observer no longer registers an alert reminder on every activation. Healthy observers hold no reminder, so a cold start after downtime no longer activates every observer at once. Reminders left by earlier versions are removed on their next tick, so the first start after upgrading still runs them once. The MongoDB storage documentation describes `minPoolSize` for warming the connection pool at start (#5677)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.6.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.6.0)): Takes Arc 22.184.0, which carries the row narrowing contract.
- **AI** ([Release v7.151.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.151.1)): Arc TypeScript proxies are generated and committed by the client that changes a command or query, never in CI. CI builds pass `-p:CratisProxiesOutputPath=` in every configuration, which is Arc's off switch. `DisableProxyGenerator` works only in repositories that define it themselves. (#1112)
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.22.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.22.0)): Ordinary appends honor an entry's generation for the event type they send.
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.22.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.22.0)): The client now depends on `cratis_chronicle_contracts` 19.153.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.5.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.5.0)): Takes Screenplay 6.14.0.
- **Chronicle.TypeScript** ([Release v6.62.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.TypeScript/releases/tag/v6.62.0)): The client now depends on `@cratis/chronicle.contracts` 19.153.0.
- **Chronicle.Kotlin** ([Release v7.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Kotlin/releases/tag/v7.9.0)): The client now depends on `io.cratis:chronicle-contracts` 19.153.0.
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.13.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.13.1)): Generated contracts and decision-read admission now target Chronicle 19.153.0.
- **AI** ([Release v7.151.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.151.0)): The worktree guidance names a writable location (`$HOME/.worktrees/<name>`) inside container workers, whose workspace parent is read-only. (#1099)
- **AI** ([Release v7.151.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.151.0)): Event types use plain `[EventType]`, so the type name is the id, including public contracts. An explicit id is only for keeping an already-persisted id or resolving a short-name collision between packages, and persisted ids are never changed. Decision 152 qualifies decisions 0007 and 0008. (#1108)
- **AI** ([Release v7.151.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.151.0)): The shared corpus takes precedence over repository-local specs and existing code for house conventions unless an accepted decision record names the rule it replaces. It never relaxes contracts, security, authorization or quality gates. (#1108)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.59.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.59.0)): A DPoP-bound token sent as a plain `Bearer` credential, a DPoP proof on a token that is not bound, and DPoP proofs combined with cookies or other credentials are refused with `invalid_token`.
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.59.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.59.0)): Introspection answers must carry a `cnf` that matches the access token's own binding on DPoP routes.
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.59.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.59.0)): The verified sender binding is carried into the forwarded identity.
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.13.0)): Generated contracts and decision-read admission target Chronicle 19.150.1; a Chronicle Kernel 19.150.0 or later is required.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.152.0)): The client event store name defaults to the program identifier or entry assembly name, normalized to a valid MongoDB database name; an explicit name is never changed. (part of #5778)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.152.0)): The kernel hashes event source ids in traces by default; set `Telemetry:EventSourceIdHashKey` for hashes that are stable across restarts, or choose `Raw`/`Omitted`. (part of #4458) (part of #5778)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.152.0)): OpenTelemetry OTLP export starts only when an OTLP endpoint is configured. (part of #4458) (part of #5778)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.152.0)): Production kernel images require storage connection details and fail at startup with a clear message when none are configured; development images keep the embedded database as a last-resort default. (part of #5778)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.152.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.152.0)): The kernel warns when the default admin password setting is ignored. (part of #5778)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.58.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.58.1)): Split invitation completion and identity resolution into smaller, intention-named steps with the same checks in the same order (part of #298).
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.58.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.58.1)): Restructured bearer route, canonical identity, tenant resolution and tenant selection code into focused rules that keep fail-closed defaults and validator error order (part of #298).
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.58.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.58.1)): Simplified token, on-behalf session, routing, authentication callback and Aspire environment code without changing observable behavior (part of #298).
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.13.0)): A default source such as `asset AssetId from $context.asset` parses, prints and round-trips, and is refused when bound until a trusted context can carry it.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.12.0)): `produces when`, `validate` and reaction `where` keep refusing the text operators when bound; one boolean property per feature remains valid.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.11.0)): Comparing a policy read with the caller identity or with a property of the authorized command is still refused when bound, until policy inputs arrive.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.10.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.10.0)): Binding a state policy to the executable semantic model is refused with an unsupported-syntax diagnostic until it is admitted. Existing models serialize with the same bytes as before.
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.58.0)): OAuth access tokens that carry a `cnf` (confirmation) claim are now refused with `401 invalid_token`, on bearer routes and on the general JWT bearer path. AuthProxy does not verify DPoP proofs, so a sender-constrained token is never treated as an ordinary bearer token. Introspection answers that carry `cnf` are treated as inactive. (part of #256)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.58.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.58.0)): The pinned Identity authorization vectors (`vectors/arc-mcp-authorization/v1.0.0`) now run as executable specs; cases AuthProxy does not own are listed explicitly with reasons. (part of #256)
- **AI** ([Release v7.149.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.149.0)): The reverse-engineering skill builds a high-level flow map first and takes intake flow by flow, and every slice, rule and rejection records the evidence it came from and the decision taken (part of #1056)
- **AI** ([Release v7.149.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.149.0)): The reverse-engineering skill pairs every time-rule probe with its negative case, stamps `$context.occurred` fields with a `given clock` in specifications, and records state-reading authorization as a gap instead of inventing syntax (part of #1056)
- **AI** ([Release v7.149.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.149.0)): The reverse-engineering skill counts a model as runnable only when Stage admits it with no unsupported artifacts and every specification passes, and writes cross-field rules as declarative `require` lines (part of #1056)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.4.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.4.0)): Queries over read models in named sinks now render instead of being refused with `STAGE-ESM-069`, and the rendered `appsettings.json` maps each declared read-model sink to the development MongoDB under `Cratis:MongoDB:Sinks:<name>:Server`
- **Stage** ([Release v5.4.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.4.0)): Stage and rendered applications pin Cratis Arc 22.182.0, which reads read models from named sinks
- **AI** ([Release v7.148.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.148.0)): The rule index in `general.md`, the review-report rules and the code and security reviewer agents now treat an uncited behavior change on such a surface, or one that conflicts with a vector, as a blocking finding (#1087)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.57.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.57.0)): A bearer request that also carries a browser session is now refused as ambiguous with a `400` JSON error, and no verifier runs. Send either the bearer token or the browser session on a request, not both. (part of #256)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.57.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.57.0)): A bearer token supplied only as an `access_token` query parameter is now refused with `400` whether or not an `Authorization` header is present. (part of #256)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): A projection whose children ordering is invalid now fails when it is registered: an unknown placement or direction, a sorted ordering without a sort property, or a sort property that is missing on the child or is an object or a collection
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): A dictionary key read from event content cannot contain `.`, `[`, `]` or a null character, and cannot be empty or start with `$`. An event that produces such a key fails its partition, which shows in the Workbench, instead of writing to the wrong place
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): Dictionary keys and child sort values are formatted and compared the same way on every server, independent of its culture
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): A plain set into a dictionary entry with a dynamic key now stores the dictionary's declared value type, for example a 32-bit integer for `Dictionary<string, int>`, instead of always a 64-bit integer. Counting and arithmetic on dictionary entries still use 64-bit integers
- **AI** ([Release v7.147.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.147.0)): The `cratis-session-handoff` skill now runs the handoff checker as its verification step and falls back to the manual checklist where the script is not installed (#1082)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.56.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.56.1)): Updated the page descriptions of the backend TLS, forwarded identity signing, telemetry and upgrade documentation for search results.
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): A Production whose spec is invalid, whose Secrets or required operators are missing, that asks to change its database or graph mode, or that asks for a Vault mode other than `None` is `Blocked` with a condition, and nothing is applied
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): The operator's chart grants it what reconciling a Production needs; cert-manager, and the Percona operator for managed databases, are prerequisites to install in the cluster
- **Showrunner** ([Release v1.20.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v1.20.0)): Neo4j servers are read through the `ServerFile` settings, which need a Chronicle release that contains Cratis/Chronicle#5771
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.56.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.56.0)): A request that presents a bearer token together with a browser session cookie on a non-bearer route is refused as ambiguous. Bearer routes consult the bearer credential alone: a session cookie beside it is ignored, never validated and never forwarded, and a rejected bearer is never rescued by the cookie. If a client sends both on a non-bearer route, send only one credential.
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.56.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.56.0)): Browser session recovery and sign-in access are limited to the matched local authentication endpoints.
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.56.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.56.0)): Client-credentials access tokens are refused at or after their protected expiry.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.9.0)): `clear` on a dictionary entry is now an error that points to `remove`
- **Arc** ([Release v22.182.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.182.0)): Cratis Chronicle packages updated to 19.144.0.
- **AI** ([Release v7.146.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.146.0)): Lane handoffs in `cratis-orchestration-lanes` now follow the shared `cratis-session-handoff` template instead of a lanes-only reference, and the planner agent keeps a state note and hands off between phases instead of compacting after each phase. (#1081)
- **Ante** ([Release v1.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.12.0)): Deployments behind an authenticating proxy must set `Cratis__Arc__TrustForwardedIdentityHeaders=true` before upgrading, or proxy sign-in stops working. Enable it only behind an ingress that strips and replaces client-supplied identity headers. Direct's DirectLobby must also enable this setting before upgrading its Ante image. See the [upgrade guide](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/blob/main/Documentation/deployment.md#upgrade-forwarded-identity-authentication).
- **Ante** ([Release v1.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.12.0)): With Arc 22.179.0, the Arc discovery endpoints for commands, queries, users, tenants and the identity-details schema require authentication outside Development. Set `Cratis__Arc__Introspection__RequireAuthentication=false` to keep anonymous access for external tooling.

## Fixed

- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.168.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.168.0)): Chronicle now removes recommendations left behind for observers and projections that no longer exist when it starts (#5644)
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.24.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.24.1)): Reactors and reducers reconnect when the server closes the client's main connection while the session's own connection stays up, such as when one kernel instance stops during a rollout. They no longer retry on the closed connection forever, logging `FunctionClauseError` in `GRPC.Client.Adapters.Mint.send_data/3`.
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.24.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.24.1)): The client no longer leaks a process for every call that fails on a closed connection, every observation stream torn down for a reconnect and every dropped session. On a long-running node these accumulated into hundreds of thousands of processes and exhausted its memory.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.167.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.167.1)): The Chronicle and dedicated health listeners reload the PKCS#12 file at `tls:certificatePath` when it changes (checked on a handshake, at most every 30 seconds). A file that cannot be loaded keeps the current certificate and is logged once.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.167.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.167.0)): Observable queries on a parent read model now update when a child is removed through a join (`RemoveWithJoin`), and a parent's cached state is correct after many children are removed at once. (part of #1896)
- **Stage** ([Release v6.1.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v6.1.1)): Calls to an extension's gRPC backend recover after the connection drops: Stage forgets the broken channel and connects again on the next attempt instead of failing every later call (#1132)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.3)): A new definition change for a projection, reactor, reducer or webhook now replaces the replay recommendation still pending from an earlier change, so only the latest one is offered. Removing an observer also removes its recommendations, and an ignored recommendation no longer reappears when the same change is reported again. (#5644)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.3)): Pull-request builds of the Workbench now reuse the yarn cache saved on main instead of downloading every package again. (#5918)
- **AI** ([Release v7.168.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.168.1)): The secrets guard classifies protected paths consistently and refuses more recursive reads of mounted secrets (#1222)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.2)): Event store subscriptions now report which constraints rejected a forwarded event and forward content whose property casing differs from the target event type's schema (part of #5797)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.2)): Protected values encrypted under an earlier key revision now decrypt after a key rotation instead of failing with an OAEP decoding error on every read (#5887)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.2)): The implicit subscriptions tutorial now uses a published package version and targets .NET 10 (#5564)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.2)): The .NET quickstart includes the namespace needed for the connection string (#5565)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.2)): The short-lived processes guide gives correct subscription and manual registration guidance (#5572)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.2)): The TypeScript gRPC contributor guide names the Node version the workflows use (#5592)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.2)): The .NET client getting-started page names `IEventLog` (#5643)
- **Scene** ([Release v6.0.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v6.0.1)): The Screenplay bridge converts nested sample-data rows into ordered JSON arrays of objects, recursively, so Scene previews show child collections; an empty collection stays an empty array, and an unknown value kind throws `UnknownSampleDataValueKind`
- **Scene** ([Release v6.0.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v6.0.1)): `@cratis/scene.editor` builds on macOS and Windows: the design-system standard's `offStandard.ts` collided with `OffStandard.tsx` on case-insensitive file systems, and is now `offStandardEntries.ts`
- **Stage** ([Release v5.30.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.30.0)): Commands run through the endpoint spec harness no longer share one set of command context values, so values written while one command runs no longer leak into the next (#965)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.1)): Constraint diagnostics and logs no longer include event source ids (#5692)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.1)): Observers no longer stall when a catch-up finds no work while still behind the event log; they retry catch-up shortly instead (part of #5351)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.1)): Disposing an in-process Chronicle connection in integration specs now ends its connection loop instead of pinging the disposed silo until the process exits (#5619)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.1)): Decimal properties declared alongside an inherited schema are now stored as decimals instead of doubles (#5840)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.1)): Retrying a failed partition from the operator tools now works after its automatic attempts are exhausted, and the partition stays quarantined when no retry job starts (#5715)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.1)): Resolving two deferred events for the same new child together no longer creates duplicate child entries (#5662)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.1)): Corrected documentation pages that no longer matched the code, and added reference pages for analyzer rules CHR0058–CHR0061 (#5931)
- **AI** ([Release v7.167.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.167.2)): The Claude Code provider's local MCP server now gives each tool call a 60-second deadline and returns the usual tool failure result when it expires, so stalled tools no longer hold request slots until the server stops (#1214)
- **AI** ([Release v7.167.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.167.2)): A tool whose schema has a `required` value that is not an array of strings now gets a JSON-RPC invalid-params error instead of an HTTP 500 (#1214)
- **AI** ([Release v7.167.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.167.1)): The secrets guard no longer refuses test runs whose filter values end in a wildcard, such as `-method 'Namespace.should_run*'`. (#1211)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.6](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.6)): The secrets guard no longer refuses reading files whose names merely contain the word `environment`, such as `*.environment.txt` and `.environment.txt` fixtures; every other `.env`-prefixed name stays protected. Run `cratis ai update` to get the updated hook. (#1203)
- **cli** ([Release v3.138.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.138.1)): `cratis env change-secrets-provider` now cleans up correctly when secrets move between two stores of the same provider: moving between two Azure Key Vaults, or between HashiCorp Vault mounts or namespaces on the same server, removes the old entries; when the old and new stores might be the same (a vault alias, or a different Vault server address), the old entries are kept and a warning names each one, so a move can never delete the secrets it just wrote. The JSON output reports how many entries were removed and any warnings (#538)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.4)): Skill evaluations now stage every rule and skill a skill depends on, so evaluations no longer run against an incomplete corpus (#978)
- **AI** ([Release v7.165.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.4)): The Pi plugin loads rules for composed profiles, including `cratis/full`, when no language is selected (#986)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.0)): Date-time event properties appended with a non-UTC offset keep that offset when the event is read back through the API, gRPC, SQL storage and compliance release, instead of being shifted into the host's local time; values written in UTC or without an offset, and previously stored events, read back exactly as before (#5907)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.166.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.166.0)): Unique constraints treat two date-time values for the same instant as equal regardless of the offset they were written with (#5907)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.31.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.31.2)): Lifecycle specifications no longer pick an unrelated read-model instance as the workflow subject when the subject's key has no matching instance; the subject is now absent unless the key matches exactly (#1135)
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.6)): Chat emoji and reaction pickers now open in the overlay container your provider supplies, layer above the dialog they are opened from, keep a scoped theme, and close on outside clicks in the document they are shown in, instead of always rendering on the page body. (#484)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.12.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.12.2)): `CommandContextValues.Empty` now returns a new instance on each access, so values written to one command context's empty values no longer leak into other contexts (#3880)
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.5)): The presentation property editor in the component picker no longer reports or previews the previous component's unsaved edits after you pick another component. (#483)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.12.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.12.1)): Chronicle 19.162.1: Registering projections from a client older than the stored definitions no longer stalls registration
- **Stage** ([Release v5.29.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.29.1)): Scene previews show nested sample-data rows as JSON arrays instead of losing rows within rows (#973)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.29.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.29.1)): Documentation pages for extensions, inbound traffic, the spec runner, rendering references and URLs now match current behavior, and the body-worker profile version, `STAGE_ROUTE_DIAGNOSTICS` and `STAGE_STATE_DIR` settings are documented (#1149)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.29.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.29.1)): Corrected the project-folder reference: supported `cratis run` commands, which declarations combine or are rejected as duplicates, and how to recover when Stage refuses a project (#940)
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.4)): `TagGroup` treats each duplicate tag as its own item: removing one occurrence of a repeated value leaves the others in place, and duplicates supplied through `value` render and can be removed individually even when `allowDuplicates` is off. (#488)
- **cli** ([Release v3.138.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.138.0)): A cancellation in the Direct MCP bridge that arrives while the request is completing no longer fails. (#537)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.12.0)): `WithChronicle()` no longer fails at startup when the application does not supply a Chronicle connection explicitly (#3634)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.12.0)): Model-bound commands are reachable in NativeAOT-published applications instead of returning 404 (part of #3595)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.12.0)): Proxy generation now discovers validators in assemblies that reference ASP.NET Core (#3873)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.12.0)): The forwarded identity header warnings now link to the secure defaults documentation (#3875)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.12.0)): Corrected documentation for the HTTP contract, commands, queries, proxy generation, identity, validation and testing so it matches current behavior (#3875)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.12.0)): Corrected query-binding, validation, response handling and React documentation (#3625)
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.3)): `DataTable` rows keep their identity when the loaded data changes: rows are keyed by their `dataKey` value instead of their position, so selection, expansion and in-row state stay with the right row after updates. Rows without a unique key fall back to positional keys. (#492)
- **Fundamentals** ([Release v7.34.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Fundamentals/releases/tag/v7.34.1)): Generated type-discovery bindings no longer fail when a referenced Cratis package removes a type; the affected binding is skipped and registration order is preserved. (#1266)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.16.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.16.1)): The Understudy backup fact is now `UnderstudyBackupTaken` (was `BackupTaken`), so its event type id no longer collides with an event type that Cratis Direct had already released. Consumers that mirror the Understudy contract must use the new name.
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.3)): The C# specifications rule and patterns now show the `ShouldContain` overload the Specifications library actually declares (`Func<T, bool>`), with an adapter example for an existing `Predicate<T>`. (#980)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.3)): The README's local verification steps now match the Yarn workspace setup and CI: one root install, the workspace check, verify, lint and test commands, package validation, and the .NET phase run separately. Paid model evaluation stays optional. (#981)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.3)): The TypeScript rule binds the paging setter at its real tuple position for plain queries and shows the shorter observable-query tuple separately. The Storybook checklist no longer asks for stories for live slice pages wired to Arc hooks. (#984)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.3)): The specification writer and the code-review checklist no longer ask for an extra race-policy proof. A rule that must hold under concurrent writers is proven from supplied read-model state, matching the scenario rule. (#989)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.31.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.31.1)): Closing an event-model board in VS Code while it is still compiling no longer leaves a file watcher behind that keeps compiling the closed board when application files change (#1142)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.164.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.164.5)): The ASP.NET Core namespace resolver no longer creates comma-joined namespaces such as `tenant,tenant` when the tenant header is sent more than once. Repeated or comma-separated header values that name the same namespace resolve to that namespace, and values naming different namespaces are rejected with an `AmbiguousNamespaceHttpHeader` error that names the header. (#5891)
- **AI** ([Release v7.164.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.164.2)): The Screenplay skills now state that systems are admitted at ESM v50 once any system in the model declares an ability (only operations, and systems in models without abilities, stay unadmitted under `PLAY0268`), that `remove via join` reports `PLAY0873` rather than `PLAY0872`, and that graphs accept a `documentation` block. (#1186)
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.2)): PivotViewer no longer leaves a Pixi application and its canvas behind when React StrictMode replays the stage effect, including when the component unmounts before initialization finishes (#493)
- **cli** ([Release v3.136.3](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.136.3)): `cratis ai install` and `cratis new` now exit with `5` (`validation_error`) for invalid options instead of `10` or `-1`, and still report what was wrong (#617)
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.1)): The documentation, README and migration guide now give the supported Arc peer range as below 24, matching the published package, and the README's Arc pin example uses 23.0.0 (#513)
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.1)): Tabs, ToggleGroup and server-side sorting are documented as shipped components and features instead of being listed as missing
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.1)): The chat and configuration editor examples now use the real component APIs
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.1)): Previously undocumented props, helpers and options are now described, including the Canvas chat props, DataTableCore with local rows, ReadModelView used on its own, SchemaEditor labels, helpers and type-color tokens, and options for the pickers, icons, markdown and toasts
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.1)): The README's table of package subpaths now points to the package exports for the complete list
- **cli** ([Release v3.136.2](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.136.2)): The Cratis CLI documentation, README and command help text now match how the commands behave, including options, defaults, exit codes and bundled component versions (#615)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.11.0)): Events appended by response value handlers now carry the command on their causation chain, also when the transactional command scope completes after the command's causation scope has ended. (#3735)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.11.0)): Enumerable observable queries close the WebSocket connection again when a custom connection handler fails to send, instead of silently skipping each item (#3652)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.11.0)): Subclasses of `WebSocketConnectionHandler` that re-implement `SendMessage` are now used for query data pushes, not only for error frames (#3652)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.11.0)): Typed command results (`Execute<TResult>`) now keep the retryable flag from the underlying result. (part of #3869)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.28.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.28.1)): Authored sidebar links now navigate correctly when the application uses pathname routing. (#946)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.28.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.28.1)): Approval decisions are rejected for workflow nodes that are not approval nodes, instead of being acknowledged as accepted. (#948)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.28.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.28.1)): State-entry commands now include the matching subject values in their payloads. (#949)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.28.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.28.1)): Rendered state machines resume in their current state, read from the followed read model, instead of always starting in their initial state. (#1104)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.28.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.28.1)): Body workers that do not use a reducer can materialize authored enum values whose generated member name differs from the authored name. (#943)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.28.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.28.1)): Restarting a semantic world no longer refuses events with `DateTime` properties, and nullable protected `DateTime` values are stored correctly. (#1139)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.31.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.31.0)): The screen composition acceptance audit now compares full recovered contract signatures, including member types and modifiers, and only accepts navigation reached by clicking the authored entry. (#1133)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.164.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.164.3)): Read model JSON schemas describe dictionaries whose values are concepts, including nullable concept values, using the concept's underlying type (#5899)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.164.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.164.3)): Observer information returned by the kernel now comes from a single consistent snapshot instead of states read at different moments (#5606)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.164.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.164.3)): Composite seed keys in read model scenarios reject lossy numeric conversions and accept valid large double keys (#5563)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.10.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.10.1)): Documents generated from C# no longer fail to load on the embedded board because of incomplete projection coverage (PLAY0284); the coverage findings are shown as warnings instead, while hand-written embedded documents and any other compiler error are still refused (#3860)
- **AI** ([Release v7.160.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.160.3)): Agent-harness image publishing now creates and verifies every version manifest (exactly linux/amd64 and linux/arm64) before moving any `latest` tag, retries each registry call, and builds the manifests from the digests the architecture builds pushed instead of mutable per-architecture tags (#1026)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.9.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.9.2)): Screenplay generation now recovers exact generated query specifications (a substituted read model set, one call to an application query and a whole expected read model) as `then query` scenarios instead of leaving them out; scenarios that cannot be stated exactly are left out with a warning that says why (part of #3547)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.164.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.164.2)): Event store subscription reminders now retry only namespace revocations that failed, and a subscription's first add also revokes observers outside its source namespaces (#5904)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.164.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.164.2)): SQL storage orders limited event sequence reads by sequence number before applying the limit (#5570)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.164.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.164.2)): Read-protection refusal messages in the .NET client now point to the correct Handle() guidance (#5587)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.9.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.9.1)): Restored the `QueryResult.Unauthorized(CorrelationId)` overload, so assemblies built against Arc 23.2 or earlier no longer fail with `MissingMethodException` when a query is refused as unauthorized (#3863)
- **cli** ([Release v3.136.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.136.1)): A builder connecting through the hosted Studio is no longer refused right after it registers. The Studio proxy removes `Authorization` on the builder path, so the CLI now sends its credential in `X-Studio-Builder-Credential` as well. It needs a Studio release that reads that header.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.164.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.164.1)): Releasing protected values no longer fails with `EncryptionKeyFenceUnavailable` when many encryption keys are tracked: the kernel now refreshes their release fences concurrently, in bounded batches, so the refresh fits within its time budget. A stale or unavailable fence still fails closed, and a timed-out release no longer affects later independent operations (#5912)
- **AI** ([Release v7.159.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.159.1)): The Screenplay command-surface reference no longer carries a stale sentence about how described handler files are admitted (#945)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.8.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.8.2)): Generated TypeScript query proxies, both normal and observable, now keep the validation severity from the server instead of dropping it. (#3636)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.8.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.8.2)): A query cache entry that has been released keeps its retention timer when the connection reconnects, so it no longer stays in the cache forever. (#3644)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.8.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.8.2)): Suspense query resources are keyed by a stable query identity rather than the class name, so different queries whose classes share a name (for example after minification) no longer share results. (#3645)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.8.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.8.2)): Mounted Suspense observable queries subscribe again after `reconnectQueries` on a shared hub, so they keep receiving updates. (#3646)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.8.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.8.2)): A command created with `useCommand` is removed from its command scope when the component unmounts, so a surviving scope no longer tracks commands from editors that are gone. (#3647)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.8.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.8.1)): The Screenplay model generated from C# projections now keeps `ToEventContextProperty(c => c.CausedBy.Subject)` mappings, at the root and inside children, instead of dropping them (#3670)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.8.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.8.1)): Joins declared inside `Children(...)` are now carried into the generated model, both with explicit `Set` mappings and with auto-mapping (#3670)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.8.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.8.1)): Computed get-only properties on read models and child records are no longer emitted as stored fields in the generated model (#3670)
- **Scene** ([Release v5.4.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v5.4.2)): Slot shape diagnostics name an action, button, toolbar element or stored presentation without an `id` as `(no id)` in both the C# and TypeScript engines, instead of `null` or `undefined` (#299)
- **Scene** ([Release v5.4.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v5.4.2)): Navigation edits on a model that declares the same outlet more than once change only the first declaration in the C# engine, matching the TypeScript engine and how navigation is read (#300)
- **Scene** ([Release v5.4.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v5.4.2)): The C# engine describes guard condition text the same way as the TypeScript engine: double quotes are written as `\"` and apostrophes are left as they are, and a negative zero is read and written as `0` (#301)
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.0)): The calendar toggle button of `DatePickerInput` now has an accessible name and points to the calendar dialog it opens with `aria-controls` and `aria-expanded` (#505)
- **Components** ([Release v4.41.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.41.0)): The Chat emoji picker and reaction picker are nonmodal button groups that move focus between options with the arrow keys, Home and End, and show emoji categories in their keyboard order (#485)
- **Showrunner** ([Release v2.11.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Showrunner/releases/tag/v2.11.1)): Cue's subtitles were drawn in the darkest surface colour, so on the dark theme the words almost vanished into the card behind them. They now use the theme's text colour. A specification guards the stylesheet against any text being drawn in a surface colour. (part of #139)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.162.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.162.1)): Registering projections from a client older than the stored definitions no longer stalls registration for the event store; the "ignored registration" recommendations are added concurrently, once per projection and version pair, and a failure adding one no longer fails the registration.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.161.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.161.0)): Generated queries resolve namespace names with the stored casing, and removing a namespace invalidates its cached casing (#5701)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.160.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.160.0)): Read models with dictionaries whose values are concepts (for example personal data marked `[PII]`) now keep the value's type and compliance metadata in the generated schema, so the kernel can release them instead of failing because the property is missing from the schema (#5896)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.27.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.27.1)): Semantic queries and read models against an external Chronicle kernel now include events appended outside Stage, instead of staying empty while status reports ready (#1059)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.27.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.27.1)): Reconciling a stopped invocation now waits until reconciliation is ready, so it no longer times out intermittently while waiting for settlement (#1134)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.27.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.27.1)): A standalone generated application waits for the Chronicle kernel to report ready before the host starts, so the Chronicle handshake no longer fails at startup (#1076)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.27.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.27.1)): A semantic world now restarts over stored events with decimal properties: new event types keep decimals exact, existing event types keep their stored schema, and Stage reports the event types that cannot be rebuilt exactly (#1060)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.159.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.159.0)): The kernel releases idle projection pipelines and their handle locks, so memory no longer grows with every namespace that has ever had an active projection observer. (#5870)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.159.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.159.0)): Reducers keep observing after the client reconnects to the kernel instead of failing with a disposed-object error. (#5869)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.6.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.6.0)): Tenant resolution from the tenant header no longer accepts a comma-joined value; conflicting values resolve to no tenant. On the HttpListener host repeated header lines cannot be detected by default; trusted ingress must strip all incoming tenant headers and inject exactly one value. (#3791)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.6.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.6.0)): Observable queries over SSE, WebSockets and streamed enumerables now authorize every update against the user admitted when the subscription started, instead of whichever identity is current when the update is sent (#3728)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.6.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.6.0)): `<Arc>` accepts any React children (strings, `null`, conditional and mixed children) without a type error (#3801)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.6.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.6.0)): MongoDB observable queries fail with a clear `MongoDBChangeStreamsNotSupported` error when the server has no change streams, instead of hanging or returning an unexplained 500 (#3668)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.6.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.6.0)): Exception-detail defaults follow the host environment and explicit options. (part of #3778)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.6.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.6.0)): Missing MongoDB connection settings name the configuration keys to supply. (part of #3778)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.6.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.6.0)): Proxy generation explains when an unset output path skips generation. (part of #3778)
- **AI** ([Release v7.157.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.157.0)): The secrets guard no longer refuses Git commands on source files whose names merely contain words such as `Credential`, `Secret` or `Token`, while real secret files stay protected (#1153)
- **cli** ([Release v3.134.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.134.1)): The Workbench applications and users lists now show the `cratis chronicle login` hint when Chronicle rejects the login token as unauthenticated, instead of an empty list (#593)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.7](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.7)): The kernel keeps at most 256 compiled projection graphs in memory and evicts the least recently used ones. Live projection pipelines keep working independently of the cache, and read-model visibility checks read only metadata instead of loading the projection. A new metric reports the cache size (part of #5856)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.6)): The kernel no longer keeps delivery correlation entries after a reactor or reducer delivery wait is abandoned, so memory no longer grows when client deliveries time out or are cancelled (part of #5856)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.5)): The kernel's encryption-key cache is now bounded: each cache map keeps at most 10,000 entries, evicting the oldest first. Expired entries for keys that were not found are swept periodically instead of staying in memory until the same key is requested again. Erased keys still cannot reappear through the cache, and a completed save always replaces what the cache held for that key (part of #5856)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.5)): The hot-core gate for pull requests labeled `run-integration` now reports its result in the same workflow run as the requested integration, so it no longer waits for a separate refresh after each push. (part of #5560)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.23.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.23.1)): The task dialog a toolbar slice button opens no longer shows the slice's kind and model name (`State Change · DraftProduct`) under its title; people see the button's title and the fields.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.21.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.21.1)): A toolbar button `for item` on a list screen now acts on the row selected in the screen's table: a button that `runs` its slice stays disabled until a row is selected, its `enabled when` reads that row, and the command is sent with the row's values. Before, such a button never had a row to act on and could not be used.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.4)): An observer catch-up or replay job step whose stored state lost its observer key now fails with a warning instead of failing repeatedly on every activation (part of #5855)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.4)): Keys that are undefined, and keys that use an array indexer value, now survive JSON serialization (part of #5855)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.63.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.63.0)): A sign-in that AuthProxy refuses during the OIDC callback is now answered as a failed sign-in and no longer leaves a session behind. (#329)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.62.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.62.2)): Paths under `/.cratis/` that AuthProxy does not serve itself, such as Arc's `/.cratis/queries/sse`, `/.cratis/queries/ws` and `/.cratis/commands`, are forwarded to the service again instead of answering 404. Since v2.56.0 every unmatched `/.cratis/` path was refused, which stopped observable queries from receiving data in Arc applications. Only AuthProxy's own endpoints (identity, providers, login, token, tenant selection, logout, link and on-behalf) stay reserved.
- **Choreography** ([Release v2.6.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Choreography/releases/tag/v2.6.1)): SQL job storage now keeps the full state of job steps whose state type adds its own properties. Previously only the base step state was saved, so a step resumed after a restart came back prepared but without its own values. Step state is now saved with the host's serializer options, including concept values and dictionaries keyed by concepts, and steps saved by earlier versions can still be read. (#217)
