- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.62.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.62.1)): A whitespace-only tenant source identifier (from a header, claim, route or host) no longer resolves a tenant, and startup now rejects tenant configurations whose `SourceIdentifiers` or `Domains` entries are empty or whitespace-only (#303)
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.8](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.8)): Worker launch no longer fails with "Could not find the file /run/agent-secrets in container" when the worker image does not pre-create the secrets directory. Secret delivery fails closed if file permissions cannot be verified, and secret-delivery execs ignore job-level environment overrides. (part of #1047)
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.8](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.8)): Worker RTK initialization is bounded and never waits on an interactive prompt. (part of #1047)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.20.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.20.1)): A toolbar button that issues a State Change slice whose own screen is a dialog (`fits dialog`, or a dialog template such as `QuickCreateDialog`) now opens that screen: the fields and labels of the form it declares for the command, without the identity the command creates, and the screen its action names with `navigate to` once the command succeeded. A slice without a dialog screen, or whose dialog screen declares neither a form nor a `navigate to`, still opens the task dialog made from the command's schema. (#1097)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.3)): A refused server stream ends with its refusal status and sends nothing further (#5834)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.2)): .NET 11 byte array properties no longer make event type registration fail because of the generated `contentEncoding` schema keyword; the .NET client omits it and the kernel ignores it when comparing event type schemas (#5826)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.186.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.186.2)): Cratis.Testing now ships a net11.0 build, so .NET 11 projects that reference it restore without NU1605 downgrade errors for System.Security.Cryptography.Pkcs and related packages (#3806)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.186.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.186.2)): Startup decision reads no longer create an unnamed Chronicle event store and use the event store the client was configured with (#3807)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.186.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.186.2)): The ARCCHR0002 analyzer no longer reports commands whose Handle returns OneOf of event-source-carrying outcomes and validation results. (#3808)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.14.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.14.0)): A set-once mapping now counts as establishing the property it sets, so a projection that only sets a required property once is no longer rejected.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.1)): Registering an `[Obsolete]` event type on .NET 11 no longer fails with `EventTypeSchemaChanged` when the type was registered from .NET 10; the `deprecated` annotation keyword is ignored when comparing event type schemas (part of #5826)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.1)): A failing partition job step now reports its original failure instead of masking it with an Orleans activation-access violation (#5804)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.186.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.186.1)): With resilience enabled (the default), the first use of each document type no longer generates a proxy type under a global lock, and calls no longer go through reflection. A host whose first requests read many read-model types used to stall for seconds after every start; a Studio event model that took 11-14 seconds to open on a fresh process now opens in about one second. Per-method concurrency limits, cancellation, and the empty results for a collection the server reports as missing work as before.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.156.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.156.0)): A graceful stop of the kernel exits with code `0`. A failed start, a stop that overruns the shutdown timeout, or a failure while stopping or releasing services exits with `70` and a Critical log naming the reason, and a lost single-writer lease exits with `1` as documented (#5813)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.12.0)): A model that uses the caller's identifier in a policy is no longer refused with STAGE-ESM-016.
- **Ante** ([Release v1.14.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.14.0)): A host upgrading from Ante 1.9.0 no longer fails to start because Chronicle refuses the changed generation-1 schemas of the setup, legal terms and registration events; their generation 1 is restored and the identity provider key and issuer move to generation 2 with a migration that leaves them empty for earlier facts (#239)
- **Synopsis** ([Release v1.2.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Synopsis/releases/tag/v1.2.4)): Repository URLs inferred from the git remote no longer include user info, query string or fragment, and only `http`, `https` and `git@github.com:` remotes are used.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.155.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.155.1)): Stopped including event property values in conversion warning logs
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.155.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.155.1)): Stopped leaking a timer when enabling read-committed snapshot isolation on SQL Server completes before the diagnostics threshold (#5811)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.155.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.155.1)): Debug builds of the solution succeed again with the .NET 11 SDK (#5818)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.60.5](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.60.5)): A person who had already completed an invitation and opened its link again got an empty "404 Not Found" page when the link had nowhere to lead (no lobby frontend configured, or a matching tenant returning to its application). They are now sent to the application root.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.9.0)): Decimal values from floating-point numbers in seeds, specification values, executed bodies and generated capture state are the same on .NET 10 and .NET 11, so rendered applications behave identically on both runtimes (part of #1047)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.9.0)): Rendering several applications at the same time no longer fails because their scaffolds shared one template engine settings location.
- **Stage** ([Release v5.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.9.0)): Pure transition bodies are analyzed against the reference assemblies for the scaffold profile's target framework, so .NET 11 applications are checked against .NET 11 (#1072)
- **cli** ([Release v3.127.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.127.0)): Rewriting AI configuration files no longer corrupts them on .NET 11. (part of #570)
- **Choreography** ([Release v2.4.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Choreography/releases/tag/v2.4.1)): Collaboration session identifiers are now scoped to the participant who connects, so a reconnect only replaces that participant's own earlier session, also across replicas
- **Arc** ([Release v22.185.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.185.0)): Floating-point values read from MongoDB and Neo4j into decimal values keep their .NET 10 results on .NET 11.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.185.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.185.0)): Fixed request handling on reused connections when a request is rejected early.
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.2)): Prevent surviving git helpers from keeping workers waiting past the git deadline while checking the remote default branch (#1126)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.154.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.154.1)): Publishing the kernel self-contained for a runtime other than the build machine's, for example `linux-arm64` from a Mac, no longer fails with `NETSDK1047` in the code generators
- **AI** ([Release v7.153.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.153.1)): The shell-writes guard no longer refuses routine read-only commands that carry pattern text: `git branch --list` and `git tag --list` patterns, `grep --include`/`--exclude` filename filters, `${var%%pattern}` style parameter-expansion operators, and glob sources in `cp`, `rsync` and `install`. Only the copy destination is checked as a write, including the file a copy creates inside a destination directory, so writes to protected paths, globs that may match them and unknown options are still refused. Run `cratis ai update` in your session's starting checkout to refresh its guards (#1119)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.154.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.154.0)): A kernel rollout converges under load: a subscribe that times out against a busy observer is retried on the same stream instead of failing the client's stream, reactor and reducer streams in the .NET client reconnect with a jittered back
- **Stage** ([Release v5.8.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.8.1)): A specification for a command whose policy reads recorded state, such as the plan of the asset it acts on, no longer comes back as unsupported ("A mapping escaped semantic admission"). It now runs against the read models the specification gives and the ones the events project. A command is denied for an asset whose plan lacks the feature, admitted for one whose plan has it, and denied for an asset with no plan recorded or only another asset's plan. Both engines agree on each case. (part of #1048)
- **AI** ([Release v7.151.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.151.3)): The reverse-engineering skill no longer points readers at an answer fixture for an application used in blind evaluations; its drift-scope self-test now runs on a synthetic application. (#1115)
- **AI** ([Release v7.151.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.151.3)): Stage admission reference: `STAGE-ESM-017` now agrees with Stage's key check (restating the key in a `from` is admitted, overwriting it with another value is refused, and `join` and `every` refuse any identifier mapping), and tells readers to key a `from` by the event's identifier property instead of relying on `no automap`. (#1116)
- **AI** ([Release v7.151.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.151.2)): Reduce false refusals for routine shell reads, quoted search patterns, read-only sed scripts, filename searches, shellcheck and GitHub CLI filters. Run `cratis ai update` in your session's starting checkout to refresh its guards. (#1106)
- **AI** ([Release v7.151.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.151.2)): Preserve sensitive-path checks for encoded GitHub API endpoints and recursive search options. (#1106)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.153.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.153.2)): A kernel node on SQL Server no longer aborts startup when its first read of a database another node is still provisioning is refused with a login failure; the read now retries like the runtime connections do.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.153.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.153.1)): A kernel with authentication disabled no longer maps the sign-in endpoints. `/connect/token` and `/api/security/login` answer 404 instead of failing with a 500 because the identity services are not registered, and clients carry on without a token (#5784)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.15.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.15.0)): Projection specifications can assert the event source of a given event without a producing command when the view defines one unambiguous identity type. When the identity cannot be decided, PLAY0273 names the event and explains how to supply it. (#1225)
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.3)): Credential-related source paths and non-secret Git path operations no longer trigger false refusals; real secret files and unresolved expansions remain protected. (#1105)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.152.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.152.2)): Kernel nodes that start at the same time against a shared store no longer fail at startup with `An item with the same key has already been added`. The development client and the configured bootstrap clients get an application id derived from their client id, so concurrent nodes register the same application, and client records already duplicated by earlier versions are tolerated (#5751)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.152.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.152.1)): Key lookup now honours key erasure for keys stored under legacy names.
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.13.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.13.0)): Redaction markers and event values whose time offset the Kernel writes as an escaped `+` now decode instead of failing.
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.2)): The shell write guard classifies only write-target words as protected references, so read-only `awk`, `jq`, `cat`, `ls` and similar commands with braces or brackets in their program text are no longer refused; commands it cannot parse stay fail-closed, and extended globs and quoted brace spans in write targets are refused (#1041)
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.2)): `ls *.csproj`, `git ls-files *.csproj` and similar read-only glob arguments to `cat`, `head`, `tail`, `wc`, `ls`, `grep` and `git ls-files` are allowed again; writes to dotfile-shaped targets stay refused (#1083)
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.2)): `jq` filters with `[`/`]` and `{`/`}`, for example `jq '{a: .[0]}'`, no longer fail the guard's parser (part of #1052)
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.2)): `node .cratis/ai/hooks/scripts/cratis-check-pr.mjs` and `bash .cratis/ai/hooks/scripts/cratis-cleanup-worktrees.sh` with their documented options pass the shell write guard in consuming repositories, while writes to those files stay refused; paths built from variables are not resolved, as the hooks README now states (#1024)
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.2)): The secrets guard treats `.env` as a protected file only as a whole file stem, so names such as `Org.DS.EnvironmentalReporting`, `Cratis.Environments.Specs` and `*.environment.txt` are no longer refused; `.env`, `.env.local`, `app.env` and `.envrc` stay protected (#1019)
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.2)): The pinned Screenplay contract is updated to 6.11.0, and the MCP tools reference now lists all 48 tools, including `list-storylines`.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.1)): Erasing personal data no longer times out when a kernel hosts many event stores; every event store is now erased concurrently instead of one after another (#5781)
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.1)): The secrets guard treats file names built from command substitutions, including those inside parameter defaults and search patterns, as unresolved, the same way it treats globs. Ordinary substitutions keep working. Run `cratis ai update` to get the updated hook (#1095)
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.0)): The secrets guard no longer refuses staging or reading ordinary source files whose names contain words such as credential, secret or token (for example `Credentials.cs`), while real credential files, including credential-named JSON files, stay protected. Run `cratis ai update` to get the updated hook (#1092)
- **cli** ([Release v3.126.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.126.0)): `cratis build run` no longer refuses a kit installed with `cratis build init` with "/scaffold/path: ./scaffold is not in the kit": the installed copy keeps no scaffold, and is now loaded as installed.
- **cli** ([Release v3.126.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.126.0)): Agents that connect to MCP servers over HTTP, such as Copilot CLI, now get the Screenplay tools in a build session: local tools are served to them on a loopback address guarded by a per-session token. Before, Copilot CLI refused the tools and built without checking the model or running the slice's specifications.
- **cli** ([Release v3.126.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.126.0)): An agent that ends its turn without writing its outcome, as Copilot CLI does when one of its actions is refused, is asked to go on up to twice instead of the build failing at once.
- **cli** ([Release v3.126.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.126.0)): Claude Code build sessions no longer load the user's own Claude Code settings. A user hook that rewrites shell commands made every command the kit permits fail its permission match, so the agent never built or tested its work; user plugins and the default model no longer change how a slice is built either.
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.57.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.57.0)): An expired AuthProxy-issued client-credentials token is now refused with `401 invalid_token` and is no longer retried through the external bearer validator. (part of #257)
- **AI** ([Release v7.147.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.147.1)): The handoff checker accepts "Files to read" entries that add a note after the path, and no longer warns about commands such as `gh run view 123` listed under unread results (#1086)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): A replay that cannot begin now says why. The job's failure reads `Could not begin observer replay: ProjectionNotFound` when the projection does not exist and `Could not begin observer replay: Failed` when establishing the replay threw, instead of the uninformative `Unknown` (#5761)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.150.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.150.0)): Updating Cratis.Choreography to 2.3.1 finalizes a replay job that was stuck starting
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.148.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.148.1)): Fix observer registrations and keep-alives being refused behind a load balancer with several silos (#5766)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.56.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.56.1)): Telemetry is now sanitized before it is queued for export. Sensitive values in spans, logs and failed native HTTP client requests are redacted, and a failure while redacting a span no longer exports the unredacted data. (#289)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.182.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.182.1)): The proxy generator no longer fails with a NotSupportedException when generating proxies for assemblies targeting .NET 11; optionality of properties and parameters is now read directly from nullable-reference metadata.

## Removed

- **Stage** ([Release v6.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v6.0.0)): The Scene conversion types that moved to `Cratis.Scene.Screenplay` are no longer in `Cratis.Stage.Contracts`
- **Stage** ([Release v6.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v6.0.0)): `themesWithAccent` in `@cratis/stage.runtime`: the brand color comes from the design system
- **Scene** ([Release v6.0.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Scene/releases/tag/v6.0.0)): The fixed theme token vocabulary (`themeTokenProperty`, `applyThemeTokens`): components read their look from the design system's anatomy
- **cli** ([Release v3.139.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.139.0)): The CLI no longer builds or publishes a builder image; the images are versioned with and released by Studio.
- **Chronicle.Kotlin** ([Release v7.10.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Kotlin/releases/tag/v7.10.0)): The `REOBSERVE_DELAY_MS` constant, which leaked into the public Java API from a private companion object, is gone
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.155.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.155.0)): The `net8.0` and `net9.0` targets of the client packages, as .NET 8 and .NET 9 reach end of support (part of #5763)
- **Choreography** ([Release v2.4.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Choreography/releases/tag/v2.4.0)): .NET 8 and .NET 9 targets; both reach end of support on November 10, 2026 (#197)
- **Chronicle.Mcp** ([Release v2.2.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Mcp/releases/tag/v2.2.0)): The .NET 10 build of the MCP server; it is an executable, so it ships only for the latest .NET (#66)

## Security

- **AI** ([Release v7.165.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.165.1)): The agent worker keeps the credential files it writes (MCP service credentials, the cluster kubeconfig and the Pi OAuth login) on in-memory storage instead of the container's home directory, and writes none of them when no in-memory storage is available (#1065)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v6.29.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v6.29.1)): VS Code event model board: "show source" opens only the `.play` files the board compiled, including files reached through `../` imports, and ignores any other path the board view sends (#1144)
- **AI** ([Release v7.159.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.159.1)): Skills no longer show MongoDB connection-URI examples that resemble credentials (part of #956)
- **Arc** ([Release v23.5.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.5.1)): Observable query connections over server-sent events now identify callers by their verified subject, so subscriptions are managed only by the caller that owns the connection
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.64.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.64.0)): Session context claims arriving from bearer tokens, invite metadata or upstream identity providers are discarded, so callers cannot spoof session start or sign-in provider (#327)
- **Arc.Go** ([Release v0.1.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc.Go/releases/tag/v0.1.3)): Bump the indirect dependency `github.com/klauspost/compress` in the MongoDB integration module from v1.19.2 to v1.20.2, fixing a reachable panic vulnerability (GO-2026-6865) in the Zstandard decoder used by the MongoDB driver.
- **Stage.Axon** ([Release v0.11.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage.Axon/releases/tag/v0.11.0)): State, typed, read-dependent and input-mapped policies, and query row narrowing, now deny access (`denyAll()`) on both the HTTP endpoint and the handler instead of rendering a weaker check (#90)
- **Stage** ([Release v5.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v5.9.0)): Updated Aspire to 13.6.5 to address GHSA-f7vh-h5g3-7jrh: rendered multi-service repositories pin the Aspire application host at 13.6.5.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.155.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.155.0)): Updated Aspire to 13.6.5 to address GHSA-f7vh-h5g3-7jrh.
- **Templates** ([Release v1.8.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Templates/releases/tag/v1.8.4)): Updated Aspire to 13.6.5 to address GHSA-f7vh-h5g3-7jrh.
- **Montage** ([Release v0.1.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Montage/releases/tag/v0.1.1)): Updated Aspire to 13.6.5 to address GHSA-f7vh-h5g3-7jrh. (#15)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.60.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.60.4)): Updated Aspire to 13.6.5 to address GHSA-f7vh-h5g3-7jrh.
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.59.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.59.1)): Tenant selection redirects now always stay on the proxy's origin.
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.2)): The secrets guard refuses `jq`, `yq`, `gojq`, `jaq` and `awk`-family commands when any word holds an environment-access identifier (`env`, `$ENV`, `ENVIRON`, `strenv`, `envsubst`, `getenv`, `PROCINFO`) as a whole token, as it already refuses `env`, `printenv` and `export -p`; names such as `environment` stay readable.
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.2)): The secrets guard protects backup and variant copies of env files such as `app.env.old`, `prod.env.local` and `config/app.env~`; `.env.example`-style templates, source, project and markup files, and names such as `Org.DS.EnvironmentalReporting` stay readable.
- **AI** ([Release v7.150.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.150.2)): The shell write guard judges a write-target word that carries `$`, backticks, `~` or parentheses by every suffix after a slash, so `$PWD/.cratis/...`, `$(pwd -P)/.mcp.json` and `~+/.mcp.json` are treated like the protected relative path; the hooks README states the remaining limits.
- **Ante** ([Release v1.12.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.12.0)): Derive `/.cratis/me` and the browser's identity from the authenticated request rather than forged or replayed `.cratis-identity` cookies, using Arc 22.179.0 on the server and frontend. Ante's forwarded subject and name header fallbacks now require an authenticated principal. (#143)

## Deprecated

- **Arc** ([Release v23.6.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v23.6.0)): Legacy tenant-id, introspection header-trust and redundant section-path aliases, in favor of their supported equivalents. (part of #3778)
